### Page 1

MediaTek

ASIC business expanding into Bytedance; 
potential high-end mobile gains in ‘27

Maintain Rating: BUY | PO: 6,200 TWD | Price: 4,475 TWD

Bytedance expanding ASIC TAM beyond Google/xAI 
Bloomberg reported on October 15th that Bytedance is close to an agreement to work 
with Mediatek on the ASIC design and turnkey service support following its collaboration 
with Broadcom and Qualcomm. This is consistent with our update in September that 
Mediatek could co-develop an AI inference chipset with Bytedance from 1H27 for tape 
out in 1H28 and see contribution from production in early ’29. Similar to Mediatek and 
xAI’s collaboration, we believe Bytedance would need the support on sourcing 
components (e.g. HBM and substrate) in addition to wafer manufacturing/packaging, 
lifting the dollar content vs. Google’s TPUs. Assuming 1mn unit demand annually & 
US$20-25k ASPs including advanced logic dies and HBM, the project could contribute 
US$20-25bn to Mediatek’s sales in ’29 (vs. BofA sales expectation on Mediatek  of 
US$82bn in ’28 with US$45bn from Google TPUv8/v9 and US$9bn from xAI’s 1-2 
quarter contribution). 
A modest tailwind in mobile as it gains share at Samsung 
The mobile chipset builds from Mediatek and Qualcomm in 2H26 are tracking below 
seasonal in 2H26 as the end demand is impacted by the elevated memory cost. While 
the market outlook looms into ’27 (vs. -25% Android unit demand in ’26), we expect 
Mediatek’s mobile business to fare better especially in the high-end segment where it 
could gain unit share by penetrating into Samsung’s flagship smartphones historically 
dominated by Qualcomm and Samsung’s own SoCs. Factoring 30% share in Samsung’s 
high-end mobile shipment of 30-35mn annual units and US$200+ chipset ASPs, it could 
be ~20% accretive to Mediatek’s mobile sales and ~5% of its total business from 2H27. 
EPS and valuation downside limited after recent pullback 
We maintain our Buy rating & NT$6,200 PO on 24x 2H27-1H28E P/E (implied 30x ASIC 
EPS + 10x merchant EPS). The share has been dragged by TPU competition concern in 
the near term but could be lifted by expanding ASIC projects and high-end mobile gains.

Company Sector 
Semiconductors

Company Description 
MediaTek is a fabless IC design company supplying to 
wireless communications and digital consumer markets. 
Before taking leadership in mobile SoC area, it had built 
solid ground in the TV SoC and optical disk drive markets. 
The firm invests heavily in R&D to seek better positioning 
in the 5G cycle, and has a complete offering including ASIC, 
Wi-Fi, PMIC etc. to address applications beyond 
smartphone, such as IoT and auto.

Investment Rationale 
We have Buy rating on MediaTek as we think MediaTek is 
ready and has the strength to brace for the upcoming 
proliferation of IoT devices and opportunities in growing 
markets like ASIC, ARM-based PC, auto, and etc., and we 
believe its solid balance sheet and cashflow could provide 
downside support to the share price. Meanwhile, we expect 
5G smartphone penetration to be a multi-year theme for 
the company, with its better competitive landscape in 
duopoly 5G era and an improved margin profile.

### Page 4

Price objective basis & risk 
MediaTek (MDTKF) 
Our PO of NT$6,200 is based on 24x 2H27-1H28 P/E. which is towards the higher end 
of its historical P/E range of 8-25x, as we believe the company's broadening ASIC 
pipeline could drive an earnings upgrade cycle.

Upside risks to our PO are: (1) better than expected smartphone demand, especially in 
China, (2) a better pricing in mobile communication segment thanks to alleviated 
competition and rising exposure to high-end/flagship market, and (3) an even more 
superior investment return on non-op side to uplift its net cash position. (4) Unexpected 
collaboration with global technology leaders leads to higher than expected 
margins/profits

Downside risks to our PO are: (1) weaker-than-expected end-market situation in 
consumer-related smartphone/TV space, (2) macro challenges including inflation/policy-
related pressures, (3) rising competition from existing players and/or newcomers in 
smartphone/TV SoC (system on chip) space, and (4) ASP/margin pressure caused by each 
or all of the above and/or rising foundry costs. (4) Bidding process for Meta Inference 
ASIC is slower than expected.

FUNDAMENTAL EQUITY OPINION KEY: Opinions include a Volatility Risk Rating, an Investment Rating and an Income Rating. VOLATILITY RISK RATINGS, indicators 
of potential price fluctuation, are: A - Low, B - Medium and C - High. INVESTMENT RATINGS reflect the analyst’s assessment of both a stock’s absolute total return 
potential as well as its attractiveness for investment relative to other stocks within its Coverage Cluster (defined below). Our investment rating s are: 1 - Buy stocks 
are expected to have a total return of at least 10% and are the most attractive stocks in the coverage cluster; 2 - Neutral stocks are expected to remain flat or 
increase in value and are less attractive than Buy rated stocks and 3 - Underperform stocks are the least attractive stocks in a coverage cluster. An investment 
rating of 6 (No Rating) indicates that a stock is no longer trading on the basis of fundamentals. Analysts assign investment ratings considering, among other things, 
the 0-12 month total return expectation for a stock and the firm’s guidelines for ratings dispersions (shown in the table below). The current price objective for a 
stock should be referenced to better understand the total return expectation at any given time. The price objective reflects the analyst’s view of the potential price 
appreciation (depreciation).

 2Q26 營收符合預期， 毛利率受 1H26 漲價提升 ， 預期 3Q26 毛利
將順利反應成本上漲與優化之產品組合 ， 毛利率預估提升 3%+ 。  
 AI 伺服器帶動高階銅箔基板需求強勁，同時驅動高階網路交換
器升級動能，銅箔基板需要更高速傳輸、訊號低損耗之下會陸
續採用 CCL M8、M9 規格。 
 2H26 低軌 S 客戶 V3 版本新型火箭搭載低軌衛星，所採用之銅
箔基板升級至 M8 等級；以及，1.6T 光模組需求量增帶動銅箔
基板需求量增。

一. 2Q26 營收與財報符合市場預期，3Q26 營收優於預期  
台燿 2Q26 營收為 143.01 億元，+42.2%QoQ、+110.93%YoY，毛利
率為 29.72% ，稅後淨利為 23.42 億元，單季稅後 EPS+7.82 元。2Q26
台燿受到 ASIC CCL 專案出貨帶動，產品組合優化，毛利率已相較
1Q26 季增 4.5% 。台燿已公告 3Q26 營收為 179.48 億元(優於市場預
期 3%)，+25.50%QoQ，毛利率可順利轉嫁且高階 CCL 出貨比重提
升，預估台燿 3Q26 毛利率為 33.21%，稅後淨利 36.72 億元，單季
稅後 EPS+12.60 元。

二. AI 伺服器帶動 CCL/PCB 產值提升，產能擴充 33% 
宏遠投顧觀察，AI 伺服器與先進封裝等需求日增，高階 CCL 市場
需求將在 2024 年~2026 年 CAGR 達 26% ， 而高階 CCL 供給端 CAGR
則是 7%，高階市場需求殷切。高階銅箔基板(包含 AI 伺服器和高
階網路交換器，自 2024 年始採用 M8 CCL 材料；2026 年 M8 材料
佔總體 CCL 市場 40%，M8 材料佔比顯著高於 2022 年、2023 年的
22%和 26%。台系銅箔基板供應鏈當中，M6~M8 等高階材料的供
應廠商為(2383)台光電為首、(6274)台燿次之。回顧 3 年來，台燿
產能擴充進程，端視泰國廠進度而定。4Q26 泰國新廠產能陸續開
出，台燿總產能為 260 萬張/月，+13%YoY。展望 2027 年，台燿泰
國廠、常熟廠初步先提升 30 萬張/月，逐步提升總新增產能達 120
萬張/月，預估 2027 年底時，總產能達 380 萬張/月，+46%YoY；預
估 2028 年底時，台燿總產能擴充至 530 萬張/月，+39.5%YoY，平
均 3 年每年產能擴充約 33% 。高階 CCL 在 AI 伺服器、高速網路交

三.  台燿高階網路 Switch 板材、低軌衛星板材、1.6T 光
模塊板材，27 年規格朝向高階型號發展 
展望 2H26，在高階 Switch 升級方面，800G Switch 採用 M8、M9 
CCL 規格，2H26 台燿將新增高階 Switch 客戶，隨著 AI 伺服器、
高階網路交換器產業發展朝向 800G、1.6T Switch 升級趨勢帶動之
下， 
銅箔基板需要更高速傳輸、訊號低損耗之下會陸續採用 CCL M8、
M9 規格。此外，2H26 開始 800G 與 1.6T 光纖收發模組(採用 M8、
M9 CCL)開始放量，AI 資料中心帶動光纖收發模組需求，400G 以
上光纖收發模組出貨量 2026 年 1 億個、2027 年 1.45 億個、2028 年
達到 1.6~1.65 億個；在中系系統廠商積極拉貨之下，1.6T 光纖收
發模組 2026/2027/2028 年估量 15~20M/55~60M/85~100M ， 1.6T 光纖
收發模組，於 2 年內呈倍數增長。低軌大型 S 客戶 V3 版本新型火
箭搭載低軌衛星 ， 所採用之銅箔基板升級至 M8 等級 ， 材料損耗要
求降低，訊號傳出完整性標準提高，對於 CCL、PCB(HDI)、玻纖
與銅箔等規格預期同步升級，推升材料成本與 PCB 產值。

四.預估 2026/2027 年稅後 EPS 38.49/66.22 元 
2026 年受惠 AI 伺服器及 ASIC 需求持續強勁的帶動下，台燿為網
通、高階網路交換器銅箔基板重要供貨廠商，將受惠 AI Server 對
於高階銅箔基板強勁的需求，因而，預估台燿 2026 年營收 618.78
億元，+108.01%YoY，毛利率 31.13%，稅後淨利 114.79 億元，稅後
EPS+38.49元 。 隨泰國廠產能釋出 ， 預估台燿 2027年營收增至 963.61
億元，+55.73%YoY，毛利率為 33.26%，稅後淨利 198.23 億元，稅
後 EPS+66.22 元。

動，一般型伺服器 CPU 廠商 Intel 、AMD 均有新品帶動新平台升級
與相關供應的零組件商機，以及攻入 AI 伺服器、AI 高階網路交換

一. 公司簡介

電路板之銅箔基板 、 內層銅箔基板 、 黏合片 ， 產品應用別當中 ， ELL
與 SLL(M7、M8)佔比 39%、VLL 與 LL(M4、M6)佔 31%、HDI 與 N-
LL 佔 16%、其他品項佔 14%。

二.2026~2027 年營運概況及展望 
1.HDI 與 M8 以上升級仍為關注焦點，2026~2028 年產能建置積極 
過往一般型伺服器用板採用 HDI 設計，強調細線路設計，CPU 尺
寸變大 Pin Count 數量增加至 2,000 只~4,000 只 Pin Count，PCB 板
面積不變之下要走向細線路化(線寬/線距)朝向更細線路設計。製
程上 HDI 經過多次壓合後，PP 在熱壓多次後，每一層熱膨脹係數
不同，導致通孔 Crack 或翹曲，製作時容易產生爆板，因此對於
PCB 板廠製作良率會是一大技術難關，而金像電、健鼎等廠商於
高階 HDI 相對具備生產優勢與競爭力，相對應台光電、台燿等高
階 CCL 供貨與升級也成為製程一大瓶頸；回顧 3 年來，台燿產能
擴充進程，端視泰國廠進度而定。4Q26 泰國新廠產能陸續開出，
台 燿 總 產 能 為 260 萬 張 /月，+13%YoY。展 望 2027 年，台 燿 泰 國 廠、
常熟廠初步先提升 30 萬張/月，逐步提升總新增產能達 120 萬張/
月 ， 預估 2027 年底時 ， 總產能達 380 萬張/月 ， +46%YoY ； 預估 2028
年底時，台燿總產能擴充至 530 萬張/月，+39.5%YoY，平均 3 年
每年產能擴充約 33%。高階 CCL 在 AI 伺服器、高速網路交換器
(高階 Switch)帶動之下，一旦，泰國廠產能擴充可望使得台燿市佔
率得以提升。2027 年隨著 Nvidia Rubin 平台將帶動 CCL M8+、M9
材料出貨攀升，AI ASIC CCL 拉貨動能呈現 50~60%高度成長，台
光電與台燿等高階 CCL 新產能釋出，產品組合持續優化。

針對 AI GPU 與 ASIC 發展進程，Google TPU 出貨量從 2026 年 400
萬顆上修至 2027 年(V8 增量)增至 800 萬顆，倍數增長。AWS ASIC 
預計 2027 年出貨量達 330~350 萬顆，增長+21%YoY 。NVIDIA 體系
GPU，Rubin 系列層數提升至 24~26L，材料端從 M8/M8+往更高規
格升級，更高層數與多層式架構，促使 PCB 板訊號走線、供電完
整性、高速訊號之阻抗匹配控制、訊號傳輸完整性，皆帶動 PCB

목표주가(하향): 260,000원

현재 주가(10/6) 112,600원

시가총액 439,605억원

ROE 19.1  12.9  12.7  12.9

주가 추이

견조한 판매 확대로 매출 견조, 이익은 시장 기대치 하회 예상 
3Q26 동사 매출액과 영업이익은 각각 33.6조원(+17.3% 이하 Y oY)과 2.39조
원(+63.6%) 기록 전망. 3Q 매출액은 친환경차 중심 신차 확대 통해 두 자릿수 
성장세를 기록. 3Q 판매량은 도매 기준 84.3만대(+7.6% 이하 Y oY)로 전년 동
기 대비 성장 폭을 확대했고, 특히 친환경차 판매량 29.8만대(+45.9%)를 기록
하며 이번 분기 매출 성장에 절대적으로 기여.  
다만, 3Q 수익성은 4~5월 상승했던 원자재 투입 영향과 ‘경제형EV 믹스’가 더
욱 확대(13.2% → 14.5%)되면서 단기 수익성에 부정적 작용하며 시장 기대치 
2.5조원을 다소 하회할 전망. 그러나, 기말 환율이 1,356원/$ 수준으로 낮 아 지
면서 전년 동기 수 익 성 악화에 크게 영 향 을 미쳤던 판매보증비 외화환산손익 
영향이 개선(1.6%p 수준) 기대되며, 관세 부담 또한 북미 HEV 확대로 전년 동
기 대비 약 4,500억원(2.0%p) 감소하면서 부정적 수익성 영향 만회 전망.  
유럽은 점유율 확대, 북미는 수익성 개선으로 
올해 북미 HEV와 유럽 BEV 중심 신차 출시 및 물량 확대가 본격화되면
서, ’27년에는 이익 성장 기여도가 확대될 것으로 전망. 미국 조 지 아 텔 루 라 이
드 HEV 신규 생산(2.8만대) 및 HMGMA 스 포 티 지HEV신규 생산(1.0만
대) 등 수 요 가 높은 북미 현 지 에 서 HEV 물량 확대 중으로 4Q에도 텔루라이
드 HEV와 스포티지 HEV의 현지 생산/판매 확대를 비롯하여 셀토스 HEV 
출시로 ’27년 북미 중심 HEV 신차 물량 증가 및 믹스 개선 기대. 유럽은 강한 
BEV 수요가 지속되면서 중국 업체들의 공세 강화 중이나 동사의 물량 확대 또
한 견조. 3Q EV3 2.9만대, EV5 2.2만대 등 유럽 BEV 수요 대응 전략 주효. ’27
년에는 K4/셀토스 및 쏘렌토 등 HEV 신차 중 심 의 친환경차 PT 믹스 다양화
로 유럽 내 물량 확대 흐름 지속 전망 
물량 확대는 증익으로 귀결, 기말 배당 매력도 부각 
’27년 동사 영업이익 추 정 치 는 11.1조원(+13.5%)으로 증익 지속 전망. 최근 
환율 추세 고려 시 내년 수익성에 부 정 적 영향 확대 가능성 존재하나, 올해 확
인된 신차 중심 물량 확대를 통해 부정적 환율 영향을 상쇄할 것 으 로 기대. 
12M EPS 추정치 하향(-2.9%) 및 상 반 기 적 용 한 코스피 프리미엄 제거(-
10%)로 목 표 주 가 를 260,000원으로 하향. 그러나, RA 설립에서의 동사 지분 
참여 및 기말 배당(’26년 DPS 7,000원 추정)에 따른 상 대 적 배당 수익 매력
도 부각 등은 주가 모 멘 텀 에 있어 기대 가능한 요소.

자료를 기관투자자 또는 제 3 자에게 사전에 제공한 사실이 없습니다. (김성래) 
저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금
합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

기아 (000270) [한화리서치]

[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

## Document 225: [373220] LG에너지솔루션(373220) Long Trading 구간LG에너지솔루션(373220) Long Trading 구간Long Trading 구간 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[373220] LG에너지솔루션(373220) Long Trading 구간LG에너지솔루션(373220) Long Trading 구간Long Trading 구간 - 기업.pdf

### Page 1

www.imeritz.com

LG에너지솔루션 (373220)

Company Brief 
이차전지/석유화학 
Analyst 노우호 
wooho.rho@meritz.co.kr 
RA 황현식 
hyeonsig.hwang@meritz.co.kr Long Trading 구간  
3Q26P 잠정실적 발표 이후: ESS ↑, BEV ↓  
LG에너지솔루션의 3Q26P 잠정실적 발표 이후 배터리 섹터는 11/3 미국 중간선거
를 전후로 Trading Long 투자의견이 유효하다. 미국 중간선거 이후 특정정당 우세와 
별개로, 미국 Ex-China ESS 공급망 프리미엄은 유효하다. 동사 역시 그간의 ESS 
사업 난제(단위당 수율, 팩 병목)는 점진적 해소될 점이다. 반면 시장의 BEV 반등 기
대감에 동의하지 않는다. EU 프랑스와 독일의 내연기관 퇴출시점 지연 움직임 및 미
국 연비규제 정책 폐지 결정으로 전통 완성차 고객사들향 BEV 배터리 출하 부진은 
여전하겠다. 미국의 BEV 정책 변화시점은 28.11월 대선으로 지연될 전망이다.

ESS 모멘텀: 한국 제3차 ESS 중앙계약시장 사업자 선정 모멘텀 
대다수 시장 참여자들은 2026년 연말까지 LG에너지솔루션의 미국 ESS 60Gwh  규
모의 잔여 수주 달성 가시성에 집중한다. 해당 ESS 사업 수주 가이던스 관건은 시기
보다 방향성이다. 단, 당사가 주목하는 연말 모멘텀은 한국 정부향 제3차 ESS 중앙
계약시장 사업자 선정으로 판단한다. 3차 규모는 1.13Gw로 1&2차 계약 규모(합산 
1.13Gw) 대비 확대되었다. 또한 언론보도상 LG에너지솔루션은 해당 수주를 위한 핵
심 4대 소재 등 전 Value-chain 국산화를 선언한 바 있다. 해당 수주 확정시, 동사
를 비롯한 해당 Value-chain에 긍정적 모멘텀이 예상된다.

투자의견 Buy, 적정주가 51만원 제시   
26년 연중 지정학 Risk, 에너지 대전환 영향 등에 전력 인프라 수요는 꾸준할 전망
이다. 향후 국내 이차전지 산업은 ‘L’ Shaped Cycle 전망, 반면 동사는 스마트카 T사
향 원통형 전지 출하량 증가, ESS 제조업 이상으로 변모하는 과정으로 산업 평균 성
장률 Outperform 가능하다. 투자의견 Buy 유지, 적정주가는 추정치 상향에 51만원
을 제시한다.

Meritz Research  2026. 10. 15

Buy (20거래일 평균종가 대비 상승 여력 기준) 
적정주가 (12개월) 510,000원

### Page 2

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
2
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
ECONOMIC INSIGHT
As was universally expected, the RBA Monetary Policy
Board (MPB) lifted the cash rate by 25bps to 4.6% at its
September 2026 meeting.
This is quite the turnaround from the 11 August meeting,
where an extended period on hold looked to be the base case.

At that time, the data had broken against the RBA’s more
hawkish view, and its published forecasts did not support
further hikes. But the MPB can now point to the emergence of
some of the upside risks to inflation it warned of in August.
The post-meeting statement was notable for its emphasis
on the Middle East conflict, noting that “global energy prices
are now much higher than had been assumed in the August
forecasts” – “much higher” is stronger language than one

normally sees in an RBA statement. The RBA’s liaison indicates
that firms are passing this on or planning to do so. The pass-
through was characterised as partial, and additional to
domestically driven inflation arising from capacity pressures. We
read this as implying that the RBA is concerned about upside
risks to underlying inflation in Q4 of this year, as well as Q3.
The RBA is also wary about pressure on both construction
costs and retail prices of IT goods arising from the AI/data

centre boom, although in the media conference, the Governor
clarified that risks there were building rather than materialising.
The broader background highlighted in the Governor’s opening
statement to the media conference, is of demand outstripping
supply. The statement evidenced this by noting the stronger
July CPI and June quarter GDP results than expected.
The accompanying statement was notable for the absence of
language about the labour market being a little tight, despite

this being the inter-meeting message from the Governor
and other staff (see, for example, the Governor’s opening
statement on 18 September) and the language in the post-
meeting media conference. Views clearly differ on this: we
note that the Treasury forecasts underlying the May budget
and the recent Intergenerational Review imply both more
spare capacity in the labour market (a lower NAIRU) and faster
growth in potential from wherever the starting point is (higher

productivity growth and trend increase in participation).
Instead, the post-meeting statement characterised the
labour market as easing “broadly as expected”, despite the
unemployment rate already rising higher than the August SMP
forecast of 4.5% for the December quarter.
RBA hikes in September, November follow-up now expected
Luci Ellis
Chief Economist, Westpac Group
The decision was unanimous, suggesting that any qualms
external MPB members might have had about the RBA’s

downbeat view of supply capacity were overruled by resurgent
oil prices and the stronger than forecast CPI and GDP data.
With upside risks being seen to have emerged, the post-meeting
statement language included that the Board “will continue to
do what it considers necessary to bring inflation sustainably
back to target, including increasing the cash rate target further if
needed”, a small but meaningful change from the “if upside risks
materialise” language from the previous meeting, and back to

the tone of the language at the June meeting.
The bar for a follow-up hike in November is low. Indeed,
judging by the post-meeting rhetoric, a November hike is now
the base case, absent a lasting resolution of the Middle East
conflict beforehand, or some other event that significantly
lowers the outlook for energy-related costs in Australia. The
bar for hikes beyond that is much higher, given the cumulative
rise in interest rates, and noting that the labour market is

easing and the housing market will likely weaken further.
The August inflation data released the day after the meeting
was in line with our expectations. We therefore continue to
expect Q3 trimmed mean inflation around 0.9%qtr, too high for
the RBA’s comfort, and upside risks for Q4. Likewise, there are
no implications for our rates view from this week’s inflation data.
Forecast changes
• We have updated our financial forecasts to reflect the change

in our rate view.
• Our labour market forecasts are unchanged on the first
decimal place, but there is some modest upside risk to our
unemployment forecast.
• Our updated GDP profile can be found on page 9.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
THE WEEK THAT WAS
Cliff Notes: searching for signal
led the correction to date, the fact that the other capitals
are also reporting price declines clearly indicates that the
correction is deepening and broadening, as higher interest
rates collide with the tax policy changes announced in May’s

budget. Sentiment effects indeed remain at play, as evinced
by the continued pull-back in investor credit growth . Westpac
anticipates a peak-to-trough decline of around 7%, with tight
on-market supply limiting the extent of the correction. For
more, see our latest Housing Pulse.
Offshore, there was plenty of data to dissect.
In the US, inflation eased in August despite ongoing strength
in household demand and higher energy prices. Headline PCE
inflation rose 0.3%mth and 3.4%yr, while core PCE increased

0.2%mth and 3.0%yr. Encouragingly for the FOMC, the
share of PCE components rising by more than 3% over the
past year, a measure frequently cited by Fed Chairman Kevin
Warsh, declined to 45% from 49%, suggesting some easing in
broad-based inflation pressures. At the same time, household
spending remained robust, increasing 0.9%mth, equivalent
to around 0.6%mth in real terms. That said, personal income
growth was soft, rising 0.2% in the month, signalling

households opted to draw down savings.
The ISM manufacturing survey was stronger than the
headline suggested. While the index edged down 0.1pt to
54.5, manufacturing has now expanded for nine consecutive
months. There were some signs of capacity constraints
emerging, with production softened in the month but backlogs
up 4.6pts to 56.4, suggesting that firms struggled to keep
pace with demand. New orders rose 1.6pts to 55.3, primarily
due to domestic demand as new export orders eased, backlogs

increased. Firms responded by lifting employment, 1.5pts to
52.7. Against this backdrop manufacturers saw a renewed
acceleration in cost pressures, with the prices paid index
jumping 6.8pts to 77.9. Firms cited higher steel and aluminium
prices, elevated energy costs, rising freight charges and
ongoing semiconductor shortages, with every major industry
reporting higher input costs during the month.
The past week’s data will have done nothing to bridge the

clear divide across the FOMC, as evident in this week’s busy
Fedspeak. Some participants argued for further rate hikes,
citing strong inflation, diminished supply capacity and policy
that is not yet restrictive. Others counselled patience, pointing
to progress on inflation and that September’s hike buys time
to assess upcoming data. The one thing both agree on is that
inflation will be the primary determinant of the timing and
extent of further rate hikes. Our view remains that one further

hike is likely this cycle to bring policy closer to neutral.
In Australia, this week’s main event was the RBA Monetary
Policy Board’s (MPB) decision to raise the cash rate by 25bps
to 4.60%. This outcome was universally expected given
Governor Bullock had already communicated that upside risks
to inflation had materialised – the conditionality established
by the MPB back in August for considering another rate hike.
Governor Bullock made explicit note of three upside risks.

The Middle East conflict was emphasised as the risk that had
“materialised”, given the re-escalation and renewed surge in crude
oil and refined product prices over recent months. The RBA’s
liaison warns this could lead to further pass-through of costs
and more persistent inflation pressures. On top of this, domestic
capacity pressures and the data centre/AI investment boom were
also noted as upside risks that the MPB remain alert to.
In a video update mid-week, Chief Economist Luci Ellis

explains why the bar for a follow-up rate hike in November is
low. We now expect a 25bp increase in November, provided
there is no sudden resolution of the Middle East conflict
beforehand, or some other event that significantly lowers the
outlook for energy-related costs.
Subsequently, the August CPI revealed that underlying
(trimmed mean) inflation rose 0.2% (3.4%yr), which was
slightly below the market’s expectation but spot on Westpac’s
forecast. As usual, the monthly detail was mixed, but the key

drivers of underlying inflation remain centred on housing-
related components, particularly rents and new dwelling
purchases. Headline inflation remained volatile, with the
unwind of the fuel excise cut causing another spike in fuel
prices. We have maintained our forecast for a 0.9% lift in
trimmed mean inflation in Q3, and with upside risks building
for Q4, a follow-up rate hike in November appears most likely.
Regarding the current growth pulse, household spending

surprised with a flat reading in August. Services spend fell back
following recent strength that was driven by major sporting
events, but this was offset by an increase in goods spend,
particularly in fuel and electric vehicles. Together with our
latest card data for early September, the consumer spending
pulse is shading a touch softer. However, another solid
outcome for business investment could once again be on the
cards for Q3. August’s goods trade data showcased another

significant spike in data centre-related equipment imports, up
$4.3bn, similar in scale to Q1. While clearly import-driven, our
modelling suggests that the local economic impact of such
investment is likely to be around half of the total investment.
Before moving offshore, a final note on housing. Cotality’s
latest update revealed that dwelling prices fell another 1.1%
nationally in September. While Sydney and Melbourne have
Sian Fenner, Head of Business and Industry Economics

Ryan Wells, Economist
Illiana Jain, Economist

4
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
In China, policymakers announced another round of targeted
support measures aimed at stabilising growth and supporting
the struggling property sector. The PBOC lowered rates on its
Pledged Supplementary Lending facility by 25bps to 1.5% and
expanded quotas for targeted lending programs. Authorities

also introduced subsidies for eligible first-home buyers. The
subsidy applies to homes with a floor area of 120m² or less
and a value below CNY1.5mn, with support capped at 1ppt of
mortgage costs, up to CNY10,000 and for a maximum term of
five years. The measures complement reforms announced in
August to improve developer financing conditions and support
housing completion. However, weak credit demand and
confidence remains a significant constraint, suggesting policy

transmission is still limited.
The September PMI data pointed to a modest improvement
in activity, although the details were less convincing.
Manufacturing returned to expansionary territory, rising 0.9pts
to 50.1, supported by stronger production and new orders.
Much of the improvement, however, reflected a rebound in
prices, with the raw materials price index rising 7.6pts and
producer prices increasing 6.2pts. Labour market conditions
remained weak, with the employment index falling a further

0.6pts and remaining below 50 for almost four years. Conditions
in the non-manufacturing sector also improved, with the
services PMI lifting from 49.0 to 50.2, driven largely by stronger
construction activity. Input and output prices moved higher,
selling prices returned back above 50, export orders improved
to 48.5 and employment increased 0.5pts, although at 45.9 it
remains firmly in contractionary territory. Overall, the surveys
suggest activity is stabilising and policy support is gaining some

traction, but weak labour demand, subdued credit growth and
soft domestic demand indicate the recovery remains fragile.
In Japan, the September Tankan survey delivered another
constructive signal on the economy and reinforced the case
for further policy normalisation. Business conditions improved
by 2pts for large manufacturers and 5pts more broadly, while
profitability indicators strengthened and capital expenditure
intentions remained elevated. Large manufacturers expect

fixed investment to increase by 11.6% in FY2026, while large
non-manufacturers expect investment growth of 11.2%,
pointing to continued confidence in the medium-term outlook.
Capacity indicators improved further across both
manufacturing and non-manufacturing sectors, with measures
of excess production capacity becoming less negative
and indicating that demand remains sufficiently strong to
keep pressure on existing capacity. Labour shortages also

intensified, with employment conditions becoming more
negative across most industries and firm sizes, pointing to a
progressively tighter labour market. Inflation expectations
remained anchored around 2%, providing further evidence
that Japan is moving towards a more sustainable inflation
environment. Overall, the survey was consistent with an
economy operating close to capacity and supports the view
that a December rate hike remains a realistic possibility.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
FOCUS ON NEW ZEALAND
Resilient activity and simmering inflation
While bumpy and uneven, New Zealand’s economic
recovery has continued over the past few months, with
activity showing resilience in the face of continued global
headwinds. Under the surface, however, inflation pressures

are bubbling away, with much of that pressure centred on
parts of the economy that aren’t sensitive to interest rates.
Economic activity getting back on firmer footing
Looking first at recent activity data, the economy has been
finding its feet again after taking a knock in the wake of the
Middle East war. That has been seen most clearly in the jobs
market, with the number of filled jobs rising by 0.9% in the
year to August. While not shooting the lights out, that is still a

noticeable improvement from the early part of the year, when
filled job numbers were falling.
Under the surface, it’s still a very mixed picture. Agriculture
remains the main engine of growth, with the strength in
commodity export earnings helping to boost incomes,
confidence and spending in regions with strong rural backbones.
That’s supported firm job growth in regions like Canterbury
(where filled job numbers are up 1.9% over the past year), Otago

(+1.5%) and Southland (+3.1%). In contrast, jobs growth remains
much more moderate in Auckland (up 0.6%) and Wellington
(up just 0.4%). That town-and-country split is likely to remain a
feature of the economic landscape for some time yet.
Looking ahead, businesses expect trading conditions to
remain resilient through the final months of the year. That
was reflected in September’s Business Outlook survey.
While the survey’s gauges of expected business activity and

general business confidence eased over the past month, they
remain at firm levels, consistent with continued moderate
growth through the latter part of the year. That resilience has
also been evident in the household sector, with consumer
confidence largely steady through September.
The resilience in household and business sentiment is notable,
as fuel prices have taken a big step higher over the past month.
The average price of 91 unleaded has risen by around 49c/litre –

albeit late in the month and likely not fully reflected in surveyed
confidence – taking it back to the levels seen during the early
stages of the Middle East conflict. Those higher fuel prices
have taken a bite out of household disposable incomes, with
spending on fuel up about 22% compared with this time last
year, even though the volume of fuel sold is down around 11%.
But even with the lift in fuel prices, we haven’t seen the same
degree of pessimism as earlier in the year, when fuel prices first

spiked. Notably, those higher fuel prices haven’t spilled over
into a broader lift in output price pressures either (at least at
this stage). Inflation expectations were steady in September’s
household and business surveys. Similarly, the number of firms
planning to raise prices fell back despite a lift in wage and cost
expectations. However, while that stability in pricing gauges is
welcome, measures of inflation pressure remain elevated.
Taken together, recent data reinforce indications that the

economy is shaking off the impacts of the Iran war.
Persistent inflation drivers and the RBNZ
Turning to inflation, the spike in oil prices earlier this year saw
annual inflation climb to 4.1% in the year to June. With local
fuel costs rising again in recent weeks, we continue to expect
inflation to remain above 3% until at least mid-2027.
However, even before the recent rise in fuel costs, inflation had
been running hot for an extended period. Excluding the rise

in food and fuel prices, inflation in the year to June was 2.9%,
and it’s been running close to or above 3% for five years now.
As we discuss in our recent report, much of the strength in
inflation over the past year has been related to administered
costs, such as local council rates, or to sectors that are less
exposed to competitive pressures, such as electricity and
insurance. Those increases have persisted for an extended period,
and we expect further sizeable increases in many of these costs.

This ongoing firmness is a key reason why we expect domestic
(non-tradables) inflation to be higher than the RBNZ expects
over the longer term. It’s also part of the reason why we
expect the RBNZ will ultimately need to raise the OCR by
more than it assumed in its September policy statement.
We are forecasting a 25bp rate rise at the RBNZ’s December
meeting and expect the OCR to peak at 4% next year.
This is a tough environment for the RBNZ. With ongoing

increases in administered prices boosting core inflation at a
time when energy prices are driving headline inflation well
above the target range, it’s hard for the RBNZ to look through
them. The RBNZ had little headroom to absorb upside inflation
surprises, even before the recent spike in oil prices.
Satish Ranchhod
Senior Economist

### Page 6

6
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
But getting inflation back to target in the face of an oil price
spike and high administered prices will not be easy and involves
some tough trade-offs. Administered prices tend to be less
responsive to changes in economic conditions. As a result,
interest rate hikes are likely to have a bigger impact on parts of

the economy that are not the main drivers of inflation. Tighter
policy will also weigh on the labour market and near-term
growth, while increasing households’ debt-servicing costs.
This is an uncomfortable but necessary trade-off. Looking
through, or not responding to, these sorts of large and persistent
cost increases would still leave households facing significant
increases in overall living costs. In the long run, it would be
more damaging for households and the economy if generalised

inflation were to become embedded in price setting behaviour.
The Kiwi has lost its wings
Adding to near-term inflation risks has been the drop in the NZ
dollar. On a trade-weighted basis, the Kiwi has fallen around
4% since the start of September, and it’s begun the December
quarter around 4% below the level the RBNZ assumed in its
September policy statement.
The slide in the Kiwi is mainly due to the strength of the US
dollar, with NZD/USD down nearly 5% since the start of

September to sit just below 0.56 at the time of writing. Moves
against other currencies have been more modest. For example,
NZD/AUD is down 2% over the same period.
Technically, NZD/USD seems oversold. In the short term,
trends in the US dollar will dominate, including any response
to the US labour market report due on Friday evening. Any
turnaround in the USD would likely see NZD/USD trade back
towards the top of the 0.5580–0.5680 range that is governing

the pair for the time being.
NZD/AUD will continue to be driven by changing views on
interest rate differentials between the RBNZ and RBA. This
week, NZD/AUD corrected higher from around 0.8070 to as high
as 0.8110 as RBA cash rate expectations were repriced following
the RBA Board meeting and Australian CPI. In the short term, the
bias for NZD/AUD could be lower, given our view that the RBNZ
will pause at its late-October meeting and that the market is

under-pricing the risk of an RBA rate hike in November.
Next week’s QSBO, due on Tuesday, will be the key event
risk, with the inflation and growth gauges closely watched for
signs that might prompt the RBNZ to pause or hike. Markets
had previously priced in a high chance of a hike at the RBNZ’s
October meeting, but in recent days appear to be reassessing
that view.  Pricing for a 25bp hike in New Zealand could be
pared back towards a 50:50 call. The 0.8030–0.8150 range

is likely to contain the pair in the immediate period ahead.
Our medium-term view is for NZD/AUD to rise as the RBNZ
increases the OCR to 4% by the second half of 2027. We’ll
update our views on the NZD and other currencies in our next
Market Outlook, out Wednesday 14 October.
Fiscal update
The big uncertainty on the domestic horizon is, of course,
November’s general election, and at this stage the polling
looks tight. Neither the established left nor right bloc has a

clear majority.
Ahead of the election, the Treasury has released its Pre-election
Economic and Fiscal Update (PREFU), updating the Budget
2026 (BEFU) fiscal forecasts to reflect the latest economic and
fiscal information and a revised economic outlook. This allows
the public to assess the state of the Government’s finances. It
also provides other parties with a baseline against which to
calibrate their spending and taxation policies.
The PREFU revealed a stronger starting point for the

Government’s finances than had been expected in the Budget.
• A deficit of $8.6bn is estimated for 2025/26, down from the
Budget estimate of $11.9bn.
• Similarly, the forecast deficit for 2026/27 has been revised
down significantly to $6.8bn from $11.4bn.
• Thereafter, the OBEGALx deficit is expected to continue
declining gradually, moving to a surplus of $4bn in 2028/29.
This broadly follows the Budget profile, but is stronger
across the forecast period.

• Net core Crown debt is forecast to peak at 43.9% of GDP in
2027/28, below the 46.1% peak forecast in the BEFU. It’s then
forecast to decline to 39.5% of GDP by the end of the forecast
period in 2030/31, in line with the Government’s goal of
reducing debt below 40% of GDP over the medium term.
Given the improved outlook, NZDM announced a $4bn
reduction in the 2026/27 bond programme to $30bn and a
$15bn reduction in the forecast programme across the whole

period through to 2029/30.
The medium-term forecasts in the PREFU should be treated
with caution. The economic forecasts underpinning the
PREFU were finalised on 21 August, when both oil prices
and, especially, bond yields were materially lower than they
are today. Had the forecasts been finalised more recently, the
fiscal projections presented today would have been weaker,
at least in the near term. Indeed, the Treasury is upfront in
acknowledging that, while the risks are two-sided, the balance

is skewed to the downside.
Aside from the obvious uncertainty surrounding the economic
outlook because of unpredictable developments in the Middle
East, the approaching General Election, and the possibility of
a change in the composition of the Government, means the
assumptions underpinning the PREFU forecasts could quickly
become invalidated.

AUS: Oct Westpac-MI Consumer Sentiment (index)
Oct 6, Last: 84.4
Consumer sentiment declined 5.2% to 84.4 in September,
taking the index back towards the deeply pessimistic levels seen
earlier in the year. Rising fuel prices and fears of an imminent

interest rate rise drove the move with a continued weakening in
housing markets likely adding to unease in some quarters.
Developments over the last month would have added to these
concerns with: the RBA raising the cash rate by 25bps to 4.6%
during the survey week; average pump prices pushing above
$2.36/litre nationally and no end in sight to global supply
disruptions;  and more weak updates on the housing market.
NZ: Q2 NZIER Survey of Business Opinion (index)

Oct 6, General business confidence last: 12.3
Business conditions picked up modestly in the June quarter as
concerns about the war in the Middle East eased. However,
cost and pricing gauges remained firm. The September quarter
survey will be closely watched ahead of the RBNZ’s 28
October meeting. Recent weeks have seen signs that activity
is firming. However, fuel prices have also risen sharply, and we
(along with the RBNZ) will be watching for signs of a broader

lift in inflation pressures. The timing of survey responses
within the sample period will also warrant scrutiny, given that
oil prices rose late in the quarter.

## Document 259: Australia’s Trade in Goods Account, August (2026-10-21)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Australia’s Trade in Goods Account, August (2026-10-21).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-10-21
Pages: 13
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

#trade #australia
Australia’s Trade in Goods Account, August
By Ryan Wells
Economist, Westpac
13:19 October 01 2026 Share
Another surge in data centre-related equipment imports in August, alongside continued
volatility in gold trade. Trade balance: $0.5bn surplus. Export values: 3.7%mth. Import
values: 5.8%mth.

### Page 2

Australia’s goods trade surplus narrowed to $0.5bn in August from $1.4bn in July (revised down from
$1.9bn previously). This result was significantly lower than expected – the market consensus was for a
broadly stable reading, while Westpac had a slight widening pencilled in. Over the past six months or
so, the trade balance has been oscillating between small deficits and surpluses, largely coinciding with
significant month-to-month volatility in the trade mix. The chief culprit behind this volatility has been

trade in non-monetary gold, among other components.
Before discussing the detail, it is once again worth noting the results for gold up front. In August, non-
monetary gold exports jumped 20%, while imports surged 91%. Excluding gold, the trade figures have
been much more stable, closer to a deficit of $2-3bn per month over the past three months, down from
larger deficits around the start of the year.
Export earnings rose 3.7% in August, bouncing back from a 3.6% decline in July. The trend for exports

so far this year looks a bit more positive, but again, this is tricky to read given the volatility present in
the data. Excluding gold, export earnings were up 1.6% in August. This was largely supported by a
4.7% lift in coal and a 2.8% increase in LNG, with further detail provided by the ABS suggests that an
increase in both volumes and prices contributed. However, iron ore (–1.5%) and rural goods (–1.0%)
weighed on earnings in the month.

### Page 3

The import bill meanwhile increase by 5.8% in August. Once again, a surge in automatic data
processing (ADP) equipment imports – which capture the investment upswing related to data centres –
were in the spotlight, surging 79% in the month up to $4.3bn. There were also an increase in other
capital goods imports centred on telecommunications equipment (+12%) and civil aircraft (+30%) –
large in their own right but dwarfed by the surge in ADP imports in the month.

Our latest estimates have the total pipeline for data centres at around $175bn over the coming decade,
with much of that being front-loaded. It is natural to expect month-to-month volatility in ADP equipment
imports as these large and lumpy data centre projects are worked through, but it is worth highlighting
that August’s jump closely resembles the surge from March, when imports also surged to around
$4.3bn. Given the scale of the increase in August, Q3 is shaping up to be another quarter where data

centre investment will play a significant role in driving growth – while it is true that the data centre build-
out is import-intensive, our modelling suggests that once spending enters Australia, the local economic
impact is likely to be around half of the total investment.

### Page 4

Elsewhere across the import basket, while gold imports also surged, it was effectively offset by a
decline in consumer imports of non-industrial transport equipment (i.e. cars), down 21.2% in August
following the surge from May to July. While still tentative at this stage, it suggests that the momentum in
EV purchases, which played a significant role in the consumer spending story in Q2, may be starting to
wane. Additionally, a further pull-back in fuel imports (–5.0%) in August has extended the downward

trend in recent months, following the initial surge in oil prices in March and April after the war in the
Middle East began. However, with the conflict re-escalating recently and margins on refined fuel
products widening, we could see this trend reverse over the coming months.

### Page 5

## Document 261: Business Checkpoint_ Looking into the logistics Profitability growth was positive in the third quarter, but logistics costs and supply chain pressures are creating challenges. 2 mi

Source PDF: all report pdf/Business Checkpoint_ Looking into the logistics Profitability growth was positive in the third quarter, but logistics costs and supply chain pressures are creating challenges. 2 min read.pdf
Report source: Bank of America
Date: 2026-10-01
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

13028181     1

Economy 
Business Checkpoint: Looking into the logistics 
15 October 2026

Key takeaways 
• Small and mid-sized businesses entered Q3 in relatively solid financial condition, with profitability growth up across both 
groups, according to Bank of America business account data. Nearly half of estimated tariff payments had been refunded by 
September, though the share of these refunds relative to revenue has peaked. 
• In Bank of America business payments data, logistics spending growth per client was up four times as much for small than mid-
sized firms. Additionally, distribution and inventory transactions growth per client has outpaced spending since the spring of 
2025. 
• Growing transportation bottlenecks are re-emerging ahead of the holiday season. Trucking capacity has tightened as driver 
availability remains constrained, according to BofA Global Research. This has pushed freight rates higher and driven strong 
transportation sector payroll growth, particularly among mid-sized businesses, according to Bank of America payments data.

Business Checkpoint is a new, regular publication from Bank of America Institute. It provides a real-time view of the financial health, opportunities 
and challenges facing small and mid-sized businesses across the US economy, leveraging the depth and breadth of Bank of America's proprietary 
data. By analyzing activity among both small and mid-sized firms, Business Checkpoint offers timely insights into business profitability, hiring, 
investment and spending trends. Such data is not intended to be reflective or indicative of, and should not be relied upon as, the results of operations, 
financial condition or performance of Bank of America. 
Businesses end Q3 in fair shape 
While profitability growth remained positive for both small and mid-sized firms in the third quarter, small firms outpaced their 
mid-sized counterparts for the second consecutive quarter (Exhibit 2). Still, there’s been a notable reversal in the profitability of 
mid-sized businesses, growing this quarter after declining slightly for the past two.  
The continued rollout of tariff refunds is serving as a tailwind to some firms' cash positions. That is particularly true for small 
firms. According to Bank of America account data, by September approximately 47% of estimated International Emergency 
Economic Powers Act-related tariff payments had been refunded to companies with less than $50 million in annual revenue.  
Exhibit 1: Tariff refunds as a share of income for businesses <$50M 
in annual revenue peaked in June

### Page 2

2 15 October 2026 
  I NSTI TUTE

According to Bank of America business payments data, the percentage point difference in tariff refunds as a share of revenue 
for firms with less than $50 million in annual revenue spiked in June but dropped to nearly zero in September. At the same time, 
the percentage point difference in tariff payments as a share of expenses began rising again relative to the 2023 average 
(Exhibit 1). This suggests that while tariffs continue to affect companies, the refund program has provided temporary relief for 
some businesses.  
Wholesale trade along with transportation and warehousing posted solid profitability growth across business size 
However, the benefits appear uneven. Such refunds have boosted cash flow, but they have not fully insulated firms from broader 
cost pressures.  
Looking across industries, mid-sized construction and wholesale firms had the strongest profitability growth in September 
(Exhibit 3). Notably, transportation and warehousing, as well as wholesale trade were among the few sectors showing positive 
profitability growth for both small and mid-sized firms last month. 
The persistence of logistics, freight and inventory-related cost pressures may help explain why profitability gains have remained 
relatively concentrated in transportation and wholesale trade. However, these sectors also have a higher exposure to rising gas 
prices within their margins (read more on this in the August Small Business Checkpoint). 
Exhibit 3: For both small and mid-sized businesses, profitability growth was positive in transportation and warehousing as well as wholesale trade 
in September 
Profitability growth by sector and business size in September (YoY%, six-month moving average)

Source: Bank of America internal data  
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

The cost of maintaining supply chains is rising 
In fact, the prices paid component of the September services reading from the Institute for Supply Management indicates still-
building price pressure for firms (Exhibit 4). This marked the highest reading since July 2022.   
Using Bank of America internal data, we identified payments to logistics and freight companies to assess a key part of supply 
chain costs. In June, logistics and freight payments’ growth per client from small businesses grew nearly 30%, though has 
moderated to 12.7% YoY in September (Exhibit 5). Meanwhile, mid-sized firms’ payments growth in this category was up just 
3.2% YoY in September. 
The divergence suggests logistics costs may be disproportionately affecting smaller firms, who are typically less capable of 
absorbing rising costs than larger firms. Larger companies also often benefit from dedicated logistics contracts and greater 
purchasing power, while smaller ones may be more exposed to spot-market transportation costs and fluctuations in freight 
pricing.

Evidence of more moving parts in companies’ supply chains 
Yet there is increasing evidence that tariffs and geopolitics are adding complexity to small and mid-sized companies’ supply 
chains. 
In Bank of America payments data, average distribution and inventory transaction growth per business client has increased 
steadily since the spring of 2025 despite spending growth in this category falling in September (Exhibit 6). Rising transaction 
growth alongside softer spending growth suggests businesses may be engaging with more suppliers, shipments or inventory 
movements even as overall procurement outlays moderates.  
Furthermore, in May 2026, the Global Supply Chain Pressure Index reached its highest reading since the summer of 2022 and has 
continued to remain elevated into October (Exhibit 7). This further emphasizes that transportation bottlenecks, delays and 
procurement challenges are becoming more common globally. While current readings remain below the extreme levels observed 
during the pandemic, the recent move higher indicates supply chain normalization may be reversing.

The trials of trucking  
As of October 1, BofA Global Research's proprietary bi-weekly Truckload Demand Indicator (see Methodology) remained below 
its historical growth signal (Exhibit 9) and the Truck Capacity Indicator, which reflects shippers' views of available capacity, 
tightened significantly, falling from 39.2 to 28.4 (Exhibit 8). This suggests shippers are seeing fewer trucks available relative to 
demand than in the previous survey. 
According to BofA Global Research, the current trucking upcycle is being driven by constrained supply rather than a significant 
improvement in freight demand. While any positive movement in demand is an important indicator as the industry moves into 
peak shipping season between late September and early December, tighter capacity appears to be the dominant force 
supporting freight rates. 
Exhibit 8: Shippers’ view of available capacity has fallen 
significantly as of October 1, 2026 
BofA Truckload Capacity Indicator by year (bi-weekly)

Source: BofA Global Research  
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

Exhibit 9: The Demand Indicator has been sub-60 for the past two 
months after 4.5 months above 
BofA Truckload Demand Indicator (bi-weekly)

Source: BofA Global Research  
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

The reduction in capacity reflects several factors, including stricter commercial driver's license (CDL) and training requirements, 
increased compliance and enforcement measures, and other regulatory changes that have reduced the pool of qualified drivers. 
This has also likely driven some wage inflation within the industry. According to Bank of America payments data, transportation 
and warehousing payroll growth per client has surged since the start of the year, especially for mid-sized businesses (Exhibit 10).  
Having to pay higher wages suggests labor shortages remain a challenge in the sector. Firms may increasingly need to offer 
higher compensation to attract and retain workers as trucking capacity tightens. In turn, such capacity puts upward pressure on 
freight rates, particularly in the spot market where small businesses may be more impacted. 
Exhibit 10: Transportation and warehousing payroll growth has surged since the start of the year, especially for mid-sized businesses

Source: Bank of America internal data

Logistics payments tend to increase in the second half of the year 
September marks a key run-up to the holiday season (read more on signs of holiday spending in the October Consumer 
Checkpoint), with spending on ocean shipping, containers, and other transportation services steadily accelerating ahead of the 
peak shopping period.  
In fact, over the past two years, the month of November has seen ocean shipping/container spending volumes well above their 
2023-2025 monthly average (Exhibit 11). The seasonal pattern likely reflects inventory restocking and holiday-related shipping 
activity. Ocean shipping volumes typically accelerate during the fall months as businesses prepare for year-end demand. 
Exhibit 11: Ocean shipping/container spending volumes were well above their 2023-2025 monthly average in November 
Logistics and freight average payments by category (monthly, indexed, 100 = monthly 2023-2025 average)

Source: Bank of America internal data  
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
Distributors and inventory payments’ growth fell ahead of holiday season 
Changes in logistics and freight spending often provide an early signal of inventory trends, as distributors account for a large 
share of supply chain-related spending (Exhibit 12). 
The sharp moderation in distribution and inventory spending growth per client for both small and mid-sized businesses indicates 
that much of the large-scale inventory accumulation seen earlier in 2025 has already occurred (Exhibit 13). In other words, 
businesses appear to be replenishing inventories for seasonal demand rather than significantly expanding stockpiles as they did 
earlier in the year. 
Overall, businesses seem to be entering the holiday season in relatively stable financial condition, supported by tariff refunds 
and positive profitability growth. However, rising logistics costs, tighter trucking capacity and elevated supply chain pressures 
suggest operating conditions are becoming more challenging.

Methodology 
Selected Bank of America transaction data is used to inform the macroeconomic views expressed in this report and should be considered in the 
context of other economic indicators and publicly available information. In certain instances, the data may provide directional and/or predictive 
value. The data used is not comprehensive; it is based on aggregated and anonymized selections of Bank of America data and may reflect a 
degree of selection bias and limitations on the data available. 
Any Business payments data represents aggregate spend from Small Business and/or Business Banking clients with a deposit account or a 
Small Business credit card. Payroll payments data include channels such as ACH (automated clearing house), bill pay, checks and wire. Bank of 
America per Business client data represents activity spending from active Business clients with a deposit account or a Small Business credit 
card and at least one transaction in each month. Small businesses in this report include business clients within Bank of America and are 
generally defined as under $5mm in annual sales revenue. Mid-sized businesses in this report include business clients within Bank of America 
and are generally defined as $5mm-$50mm in annual sales revenue. 
Unless otherwise stated, data is not adjusted for seasonality, processing days or portfolio changes, and may be subject to periodic revisions.  
Revenue tiers are determined by the combination of following factors: 1) stated revenue on small businesses credit applications, 2) actual 
account inflow into Bank of America Deposit Accounts, and 3) third party revenue estimation.  
The alternative hiring indicator consists of payments from Bank of America business clients to business-focused hiring firms which include both 
direct deposits through Automated Clearing House (ACH) and payments via credit and debit cards. 
Supply chain-related spending is comprised of payments to logistics, freight, distribution, inventory, delivery service, and trucking companies. 
These are identified from merchant names and MCC codes. 
BofA Global Research’s trucking indicators are a bi-weekly diffusion-style survey of shipper demand expectations over the next 0–3 months, 
built from responses from surveyed shippers. 
Data regarding merchants who receive payments are identified and classified by the Merchant Categorization Code (MCC) defined by financial 
services companies. The data are mapped using proprietary methods from the MCCs to the North American Industry Classification System 
(NAICS), which is also used by the Census Bureau, in order to classify spending data by subsector. Spending data may also be classified by other 
proprietary methods not using MCCs.  
Additional information about the methodology used to aggregate the data is available upon request.

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -29.18 điểm tương ứng -1.68%, với thanh khoản suy 
giảm. Dầu khí, Bảo hiểm và Dịch vụ phân phối là các nhóm ngành có mức giảm rõ rệt nhất 
trong tuần. Khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng so với xu hướng của tuần trước với giá trị -3,013 tỷ 
đồng, tập trung ở các mã MCH, VIC và FPT; mặt khác, tự doanh đảo chiều mua ròng ở mức 
887 tỷ đồng.

Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại đảo chiều bán ròng -2,021 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần 
qua và đang duy trì vị thế bán lũy kế -4,384 hợp đồng. Tự doanh đảo chiều bán ròng -
4,275 hợp đồng và đang duy trì trạng thái lũy kế bán ròng -7,815 hợp đồng.

TTCK Việt Nam tiếp tục điều chỉnh tuần thứ tư liên tiếp, khi VN-Index lùi về đóng cửa 1.706 
điểm - giảm khoảng 1,7% so với tuần trước và đang dao động quanh vùng hỗ trợ tâm lý 
1.700 điểm, cũng là vùng đáy cũ của tháng 8. Thanh khoản thị trường tiếp tục suy yếu, 
phản ánh tâm lý thận trọng cao độ trong bối cảnh thị trường thiếu vắng thông tin hỗ trợ 
trong nước, trong khi các yếu tố bên ngoài như xung đột tại Iran, và chi phí vốn toàn cầu 
chưa hạ nhiệt tiếp tục đè nặng tâm lý nhà đầu tư. Khối ngoại duy trì chuỗi bán ròng kéo 
dài nhiều phiên liên tiếp, nối dài đà rút vốn đã lên tới xấp xỉ 95 nghìn tỷ đồng từ đầu năm. 
Trong một, hai tuần tới, mùa báo cáo kết quả kinh doanh quý 3 được kỳ vọng là chất xúc 
tác giúp thị trường giao dịch cân bằng trở lại.

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX tiếp tục hình thành mẫu nến giảm điểm tiêu cực trên đồ thị tuần, đánh dấu tuần 
giảm thứ 4 liên tiếp, cho thấy áp lực phân phối vẫn đang có phần lấn át. Trên khung ngày, 
tuy chỉ số đã có nỗ lực phục hồi trở lại trong 3 phiên đan xen, đặc biệt là phản ứng trong 
phiên cuối tuần khi chạm trendline trung hạn, nhưng việc chỉ có thể hình thành các mẫu 
nến "spinning" hàm ý đà tích lũy chưa thực sự thuyết phục. Trong một vài phiên kế tiếp, 
VNINDEX có thể xuất hiện nhịp hồi kỹ thuật từ vùng hỗ trợ, tương ứng với cạnh dưới tam 
giác, nhưng sau khi chớm phá vùng hỗ trợ quanh 1710 và hiện chỉ còn một số ít các mã giữ 
được xu hướng tăng tính từ đáy tháng 7, xu hướng ngắn hạn đang trở nên khá trung tính.

(Ghi chú: NĐT có thể dựa theo nhận định xu hướng thị trường để tham khảo khuyến nghị 
hành động tương ứng trong bảng phía trên)

• 非核心消費板塊獲利前景近
期蒙上陰霾。首先，郵輪業
雖受惠於旅遊需求韌性而上
調 2026 財年預測，但高利
率與高油價恐使後市持續承
壓；其次，運動鞋服產業受
到大中華區市場衰退與新興
小品牌崛起雙重打擊，面臨
營收與獲利雙雙下滑的困境
，短期內組織重整效益恐難
顯現。再者，在即將迎來的
財報季前夕，市場共識普遍
預期 3Q26 的增長將陷入停
滯。
《市場共識預期3Q26非核心消費板塊成長停滯》
美3Q26財報季將陸續揭曉 ，目前市場共識預期標
普500指數各產業板塊第三季的獲利成長存在較大
差異，其中資訊科技和能源板塊將推動指數整體獲
利成長，預期非核心消費板塊整體每股盈餘將不會
成長。另外，零星個股捎來截至 8月底的季度業績
也顯示好壞參半的結果。
《郵輪旅遊需求仍具韌性，燃油成本仍是潛在風險》
嘉年華郵輪 (CCL)3Q26財年(截至8月底)營收年增
3.5%，每股盈餘持平 ，淨收益率超預期 ，主要貢
獻來自船上及其它收入，然因燃料成本大增36%，
完全抵銷了營運效率與成本控制的效益 ，導致調整

後EBITDA持平。管理層上調2026年全年獲利展望 ，
且公司表示 2027年訂位量及訂位價格均創下歷史
新高，目前已售出約半數 2027年艙位 ，顯示旅遊
需求仍具韌性。但市場對其未來半年的獲利預期偏

《運動鞋服Nike轉型成效慢，獲利展望不佳》
Nike1Q27財年(2026/6~2026/8)營收與每股盈餘
皆衰退。運動休閒系列、Jordan品牌和大中華區市
場下滑 ，成為主要逆風 。管理層預期 2027財年營

於市場預期 。即便Nike推出名為 Pace營運重整計
畫，但效益明顯無法在短期內看到 。其中一個原因
是Nike的市場主導地位已不如前，小型品牌如今已

轉變時(品牌忠誠度降低與多品牌嘗試 )，如何收復
市佔率就成為一項艱巨且持久的挑戰。
《2026 美年底消費總額預期創新高 ，逾半增長靠
「通膨」，電商與生成式AI成雙引擎》
近期已有多家研調機構針對美國2026年年底消費
旺季出具預測報告。整體來看，各大機構預測的銷
售總額皆創下歷史新高 ，但其背後的共同隱憂是
「通膨」，直言超過一半的名目增長率是由物價上
漲驅動。這使得消費者普遍展現「價格敏感、主動
尋找折扣、切換品牌以省錢」的價值導向行為。電
商增速(網購)依然大幅碾壓實體零售。所有機構也
都點出生成式AI在2026年成為主流購物工具。
資料來源：Mastercard， 2026/9/23，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10/13

機構預測2026年底消費旺季
各家機構重點分述如下
Mastercard ： 預 期 假日消費成長近半因物價上漲 ，
今年主因能源與AI建設成本增加，去年則受關稅影
響。鑑於柴油價格持續高企可能推高零售商的運輸
成本，並對年底的整體消費價格構成上行壓力，因
此值得密切關注。高收入家庭因財富成長、增加自
由支配支出，成為近年消費成長的重要引領者， 然
而，受益於勞動市場穩定、薪資成長與健康資產負
債表，各收入群體亦持續穩定貢獻，消費仍由廣大
家庭共同支撐。
Adobe：預計今年假期季節將推出極具競爭力的折

扣，雖然消費者歷來都熱衷於利用節日促銷活動購
買禮物或添加最新電子產品，但許多人現在更傾向
於利用折扣囤積生活必需品。另，今年假期季節美
國零售網站的人工智慧流 量 將年增130%。預計感
恩節的增幅最大(年增159%)，其次是黑色星期五(
年成長 95%)和網路星期一(年增88%)。
德勤(Deloitte) ： 消費者仍然重視為親朋好友營造
特別的節日氛圍，同時也在精打細算地消費。為了
更好地利用每一分錢，觀察到各個收入階層的消費
者都在追求性價比，例如在不同品牌和零售商之間
切換，以及利用促銷活動來控制支出。

## Document 501: スペイン、前倒しの総選挙へ ～政権交代なら住宅・移民政策を軌道修正～

Source PDF: all report pdf/スペイン、前倒しの総選挙へ ～政権交代なら住宅・移民政策を軌道修正～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-10-06
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Europe Trends

2026 年 10 月６日

1/3 
スペイン、前倒しの総選挙へ 
～政権交代なら住宅・移民政策を軌道修正～ 
 経済調査部 首席エコノミスト  田中 理 
（℡：050-5474-7494）

（要旨） 
○ スペインのサンチェス首相は10 月５日、 議会を解散し、11 月 29 日に前倒し総選挙を実施す
ると発表した。少数政権による議会運営は予算や住宅政策を巡って行き詰まり、カタルーニ
ャ独立派政党との関係悪化も政権運営を難しくしていた。足元の世論調査では中道右派の国
民党（ＰＰ）が第１党、右派ポピュリストのボックス（Vox）が第３党につけ、ＰＰ単独では
なくVox の協力を前提とした政権発足の可能性が高い。 政権交代となれば、 住宅政策は規制 ・
借家人保護重視から供給拡大や規制緩和へ軸足を移し、 移民政策も厳格化する可能性がある。
特に Vox の影響力が強まれば、人口・労働力の増加に支えられてきたスペインの成長モデル
や、ＥＵ・カタルーニャとの関係にも変化が及ぶ可能性がある。

■少数政権の行き詰まり、住宅問題が解散の引き金に 
スペインのサンチェス首相は10 月５日、議会を解散し、11 月 29 日に前倒し総選挙を実施する
と発表した。2027 年までの議会任期を全うするとしてきた従来の方針を転換したもので、2023 年
の総選挙後に続いてきた少数政権による不安定な議会運営はひとまず終止符を打つことになる。
今回の解散・総選挙は、少数与党による議会運営の行き詰まりに、与党関係者を巡る様々な疑
惑、カタルーニャ独立派の地域政党との関係悪化、住宅危機への国民の不満などが重なり、政府
が重要政策を成立させることが難しくなったことが背景にある。 
2023 年の総選挙後に発足したサンチェス政権は、中道左派の「社会労働党（PSOE）」と左派の
「スマール（Sumar）」による連立政権だが、両党だけでは下院の過半数を確保できない。そのた
め、政権発足時には、「カタルーニャ共和主義左翼（ＥＲＣ）」、「ともにカタルーニャのために
（Junts）」、「バスク民族党（ＰＮＶ）」、「バスク統一党（Bildu）」など、カタルーニャやバスクを
中心とする地域政党から協力を取り付けた。なかでも、サンチェス政権の存続に重要な役割を果
たしてきたのがJuntsである。2023 年の政権発足時には、2017 年のカタルーニャの独立運動を巡
って有罪判決を受けた政治家や活動家を対象とする恩赦を条件に、サンチェス首相の続投に協力
したが、連立政権には参加せず、その後も個々の法案毎に賛否を判断してきた。このため、少数
の議席を持つ地域政党が政府の法案成立を左右する不安定な状況が続いてきた。 
政権運営の機能不全を象徴しているのが予算だ。政府は2023 年予算を最後に新たな予算を成立

### Page 2

2/3 
させることができず、過去３年は既存予算の延長を余儀なくされてきた。2026 年７月には、2027
年予算編成の前提となる財政安定化計画も、中道右派の「国民党（ＰＰ） 」、右派ポピュリストの
「ボックス（Vox）」、Juntsなどの反対で議会で否決された。閣僚経験者、党幹部、首相の家族な
ど、与党関係者を巡る疑惑が相次いで表面化したことも、政権への風当たりを強めた。それで
も、サンチェス首相は2027 年までの議会任期を全うする方針を維持してきた。 
こうした状況を大きく変えたのが住宅問題だ。スペインでは人口や世帯数の増加に住宅供給が
追いつかず、住宅価格と家賃の上昇が大きな社会問題となっている。９月下旬にマドリードで87
歳の女性が長年暮らしてきた住居から立ち退かされたことを契機に抗議活動が広がり、住宅問題
が一気に政治争点として浮上した（詳細は10 月１日付けレポート「スペイン高成長の裏で深まる
住宅危機」を参照されたい）。政府は立ち退き停止措置の延長や賃貸市場の規制強化などを盛り込
んだ緊急政令を議会に提出したが、ＰＰやVoxなどの野党勢力に加えて、Juntsも反対に回った結
果、承認されなかった。Juntsは借家人保護や賃貸規制の強化が住宅所有者を賃貸市場から退出さ
せ、結果的に供給不足や家賃上昇を深刻化させると主張した。サンチェス首相が総選挙の前倒し
を決断した背景には、こうした議会運営の行き詰まりがある。同時に、住宅危機への国民の関心
が急速に高まるなか、住宅政策を選挙の中心的な争点に据えることで、劣勢の挽回を図る狙いも
あるとみられる。

■政権交代の可能性が高い、Voxの政策関与に注目 
 各種の世論調査では、ＰＰが30％台前半の支持を集めてリードし、それを20％台後半のPSOEが
追い、Voxが20％近くで三番手につけている（図）。PSOEが現政権で連立を組むSumarは５～６％前
後で低迷している。前倒し総選挙の正式発表後に実施された世論調査はまだ公表されていない。
このまま世論調査に近い結果となれば、PSOEがＰＰに政権を明け渡すことになりそうだ。だが、
ＰＰも単独で過半数を確保するのは難しく、安定政権の樹立にはVoxの協力が必要となる公算が大
きい。Voxが連立政権の一員に加わるのか、それとも閣外からＰＰ政権を支えるのかによって、政
策決定への関与の度合いが変わってくる。既に複数の州政府で、ＰＰが主導する政権にVoxが連立
パートナーとして参加している。連立政権に加わる場合、ＰＰのフェイホー党首が首相に就任
し、Voxが副首相や閣僚ポストを獲得する形が考えられる。閣外協力にとどまる場合、政権発足時
や重要法案の審議でＰＰ政権を支えることになる。 
ＰＰが主導する政権が誕生した場合、PSOE政権の公的介入路線が軌道修正され、減税や規制緩
和を通じた経済活性化を目指す可能性が高い。ＰＰのフェイホー党首は、年間20 万戸規模の住宅
建設、土地供給の拡大、建設手続きの簡素化、住宅関連税制の軽減などを掲げている。移民政策
の転換も予想される。サンチェス政権は移民の受け入れに比較的寛容で、移民による人口と労働
力の増加は近年のスペインの高成長を支える重要な要因となってきた。移民規制が強まれば、住
宅需要への圧力を幾分弱める可能性がある反面、労働供給の増加ペースが鈍化する。観光、建
設、農業、介護など移民労働力への依存度が高い業種では人手不足や賃金上昇圧力が強まること
が予想される。特にVoxは、不法移民の強制送還、移民への給付制限、長期滞在資格の厳格化な

### Page 3

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
3/3 
ど、移民政策の強化を主張している。ＰＰとVoxが連立を組む州政府では、住宅や公共サービスで
スペイン国民を優先する考えが盛り込まれた例もある。 
 ＰＰが主導する政権の誕生は、カタルーニャ独立派との対話路線が転換され、独立の火種を抱
えるカタルーニャ州政府との関係を再び悪化させる恐れがある。ＰＰがかつて率いた政権では、
領土的一体性や法の執行を重視し、カタルーニャの独立問題に厳しく対処した。次期政権とカタ
ルーニャ州政府との緊張が再び高まれば、金融市場でスペインの政治リスクが意識される可能性
がある。 
 Voxの政権参加によるＥＵ関係の悪化を懸念する声もあるが、ＰＰ自体は長年スペインの政権を
率いてきた親ＥＵの中道右派政党で、政権交代によってスペインのＥＵ政策が大幅に変わること
は想定しにくい。ただ、Voxの政策面での影響力が増すことで、移民政策、気候変動政策、農業政
策などでは、スペイン政府とＥＵ機関との摩擦が増える可能性がある。 
現時点でスペインの政治リスクをフランスと同列に扱う必要はない。スペイン経済は相対的に
高い成長を維持しており、今回の解散も財政危機や景気後退を背景としたものではない。むし
ろ、市場にとって重要なのは、総選挙によってこれまでの議会の機能不全が解消され、安定した
政権が成立するかどうかである。次期政権が安定多数を確保すれば、予算を成立させられなかっ
た現政権に比べ、短期的には政策の予見可能性が改善する。一方で、Voxの影響力が大きくなるほ
ど、移民政策、気候変動対策、カタルーニャ政策、ＥＵ政策での変化が大きくなる。特に移民政
策の厳格化は、人口増加に支えられてきたスペインの成長モデルに中期的な変化をもたらす。

以 上

　た（SALF）、カタルーニャ共和主義左翼（ERC）、ともにカタルーニャ
　のために（Junts）、バスク統一党（Bildu）、バスク民族党（PNV）、
　ガリシア人民ブロック（BNG）、カナリア人民党（CC）。

## Document 530: 中国移动前三季度利润达1062亿元，其他业务收入增长近10%｜财报见闻

Source PDF: all report pdf/中国移动前三季度利润达1062亿元，其他业务收入增长近10%｜财报见闻.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-20
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

中国移动 2026 年前三季度实现营收 7803 亿元、利润 1062 亿元，⽤⼾规模持续扩⼤。其他
业务收⼊同⽐增⻓ 9.9% ，经营现⾦流同⽐增⻓ 15% ，财务结构保持稳健，转型与基础设施
建设稳步推进。
摘要：
中国移动 2026 年前三季度业绩保持稳健，⽤⼾规模持续扩⼤，转型业务动能增强，算⼒基础设
施投⼊加快推进，经营现⾦流明显改善，整体财务结构保持稳健。
周⼆，中国移动发布的 2026 年前三季度未经审计业绩公告显⽰，集团营运收⼊达 7803 亿元，利
润为 1062 亿元， EBITDA 为 2475 亿元。收⼊结构⽅⾯，主营业务收⼊为 6577 亿元，其他业务收
⼊同⽐增⻓ 9.9% ⾄ 1226 亿元。
公司加快向科技服务企业转型，宽带与 5G ⽤⼾规模稳步提升，其他业务收⼊保持较快增⻓，在
建⼯程规模较年初增⻓约 57.9% ，展现出较强的经营韧性和转型潜⼒。
其他业务增⻓亮眼，转型布局加速推进
前三季度，中国移动营运收⼊同⽐减少约 144 亿元。
成本⽅⾯，销售产品成本由去年同期的 1095 亿元增⾄ 1209 亿元，同⽐增⻓约 10.4% 。⽹络运营

及⽀撑成本、员⼯薪酬等主要⽀出有所下降。
同期，集团营运利润由去年同期的 1234 亿元降⾄ 1057 亿元，同⽐下降 14.3% ，降幅⾼于营收。
股东应占利润率由 14.5% 降⾄ 13.6% ， EBITDA 率由 33.4% 降⾄ 31.7% 。
其他业务收⼊同⽐增⻓ 9.9% ⾄ 1226 亿元，成为收⼊结构中的增⻓来源，占集团总营收的⽐重仍
约为 15.7% 。
中国移动表⽰，将继续推进通信⽹、算⼒⽹、智能⽹协同布局，加快向科技服务企业转型。与
此同时，集团在建⼯程规模由年初的 618 亿元增⾄ 976 亿元，增⻓约 57.9% ，显⽰相关基础设施
建设仍在推进。
经营现⾦流增⻓，资产负债表保持稳健

## Document 545: 企業物価指数2026年9月～コメを含む農林水産物が24年4月以来の前年比マイナスに転じる～

1. 国内企業物価の前年比上昇率は前月から縮小 
日本銀行が10 月13 日に発表した企業物価指数
によると、2026 年 9 月の国内企業物価は前月比▲
0.3％と 2 ヵ月連続のマイナスとなり、前年比で
は6.8％（8 月：7.6％）と前月から伸びが縮小し
た。 
内訳をみると、23 類別中 22 類別が前年比で上
昇、1 類別で低下となった。コメを含む農林水産
物は、 前月比▲8.8％と大幅に低下し、前年比では
▲13.3％（8 月：同1.5％）と24 年 4 月以来の前
年比マイナス に転じ 、国内企業物価を押し下げ
た。集荷業者が生産者に支払う概算金の引き下げ
や民間在庫の積み上がりを背景に、玄米は前年比
▲31.9％（8 月：同11.9％）とマイナスに転じた
ほか、精米は前年比▲16.2％（8 月：同▲5.0％）
とマイナス幅が拡大した。 
政府による電気 ・ 都市ガス代の支援策が26 年7～9 月使用分（国内企業物価への反映は8～10 月）
で実施されているものの、燃料費が前年よりも上昇しているため、電気・都市ガス・水道（夏季電
力料金調整後）は前年比 11.5％（8 月：同 9.1％）と前月から伸びが拡大した。都市ガスが前年比
11.2％（8 月：同 4.0％）と伸びが大幅に拡大したほか、事業用電力（8 月：前年比 9.3％→9 月：
同10.7％） 、上水道（8 月：前年比4.5％→9 月：同4.7％）も伸びが拡大した。

### Page 2

2. 円ベースの輸入物価は前年比 20％台の高水準が続く 
26 年9 月の契約通貨ベースの輸入物価は、前年比17.9％（8 月：同 16.8％）と前月から伸びが
拡大した。内訳をみると、液化天然ガスが前年比43.3％（8 月：同28.0％）、ナフサが同44.3％
（8 月：同28.5％）、ジェット燃料油が同76.7％（8 月：同56.5％）といずれも前月から伸びが大
幅に拡大したことなどから、石油・石炭・天然ガスは前年比36.1％（8 月：同32.8％）と前月か
ら伸びが拡大した。 
堅調なＡＩ関連需要を背景に、25 年秋頃から伸びが急拡大してきた半導体メモリは、モス型メ
モリ集積回路が前年比316.2％（8 月：同349.4％） 、記録メディアが同101.2％（8 月：同
109.8％）と高い伸びが続いている。また、メモリを使用する携帯電話機が前年比11.6％（8 月：
同▲2.1％）とプラスに転じたことなどから、電気・電子機器は前年比22.5％（8 月：同21.8％）
と伸びがやや拡大した。 
9 月の為替相場は対ドルで156 円台（前月比▲1.5％）となった。8 月から円高・ドル安が進ん
だこともあり、円ベースでは前年比24.5％（8 月：同 25.1％）と前月から伸びが縮小した。

3. 先行きの国内企業物価上昇率は、緩やかに低下するものの、高水準で推移 
先行きの国内企業物価上昇率は、緩やかに低下するものの、高水準で推移することが予想される。
農林水産物は前年比マイナスが続くことが見込まれるため、 国内企業物価の上昇率を押し下げる公
算が大きい。一方、川上の輸入物価（円ベース）は、26 年 3 月以降、前年比 20％を超える上昇が
続いている。ドル円レートは、9 月中旬に一時1 ドル 153 円台まで円高が進んだが、執筆時点では

### Page 1

十月 11, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  Powered by the EFA Platform 1

動態更新 電子 ‧ 台灣

緯穎 
(6669.TW /6669 TT)

客製化 AI ASIC 持續驅動 2027 年獲利強勁成長 
重要訊息 
緯穎 9 月與 3Q26 營收優於我們預期。我們預期 4Q26 營收將持續季增及年
增，並預期在 ASIC AI 伺服器與通用型伺服器營收增加，加上新客戶與新專
案帶動下，2027  年獲利展望穩健。  
評論及分析 
3Q26 營收優於預期；產品組合改善有利利潤率。緯穎 9 月營收達 1,726 億元 
(月增 20%、年增 100%)，優於預期，帶動 3Q26 營收達 4,346.2 億元 (季增 
56%)，較我們預估高 16%。此強勁表現應歸因於通用伺服器營收較佳，及  9 
月 ASIC AI 伺服器營收成長。9 月 ASIC AI 營收占比已超過營收的 50%，而通
用伺服器則維持穩定，3Q26 通用伺服器營收占比仍高於 50%，季增預估超過
20%。AWS (美) Trainium3 (T3) 占 3Q26 A I 伺服器(估 5k 櫃以上)營收幾乎全
部，而 9 月營收顯著增加  (估 3k 櫃以上)。營收成長及產品組合改善將有利  
3Q26利潤率。我們因而上修 3Q26 EPS 預估至 42.76 元 (季增 59%)。 
AI 專案與通用型伺服器需求帶動  2027 年獲利展望穩健。我們預期緯穎將於  
2027 年受惠於通用型伺服器與 AI 伺服器的需求及營收成長，主因多項 AI 專
案啟動所帶動。T3 Max (液冷) AI 伺服器自 4Q26 起開始出貨，1Q27 將量產。
預估液冷機櫃 ASP 可能為氣冷 PDS 機種的 2-3 倍，且緯穎應為 T3 Max 的唯一 
ODM。此外，緯穎供應 Meta (美) 的 AMD (美) Helios 機櫃，及供應 Oracle (美) 
的 VR200 機櫃，這些 GPU AI 相關伺服器預計自 4Q26 末開始貢獻營收，並於 
2027 年貢獻更為顯著。但 GPU 專案的變數在於銷售交易條件採買賣制 (buy-
and-sell) 或寄售制 (consignment)。再加上對新 Neocloud 客戶的潛在出貨，緯
穎明年 AI 伺服器營收擴張應相當顯著。而受惠於 代理式 AI與推論需求，及潛
在新增 CSP 客戶，我們預期 2027 年通用伺服器營收亦將成長，並為利潤率提
供支撐。我們預估 4Q26 營收將季增約 10%，毛利率約為 8.5%。受惠於營收
表現更強，我們上修 2026 年 EPS 預估至 139.76 元。考量 2027 年營收展望更
為樂觀，我們亦將  2027 年 EPS 預估上修至 223.38 元 (年增 60%)。整體而
言，我們認為客製化 AI 伺服器的強勁需求，將為緯穎帶來更多商機，如來自 
OpenAI (美) 與 Meta 等業者的訂單。 
投資建議  
我們維持緯穎「增加持股」評等，並將目標價重設 為 3,000 元 (除權後)，基
於 2027 年完全稀釋每股盈餘 200.01 元的 15 倍 (以 6.2 億股股本計算)。 
投資風險 
零組件供應緊缺；AI伺服器因寄售導致價格變化；AI伺服器專案遞延。

圖 9：公司概況  圖 10：Microsoft、Meta與 AWS為緯穎主要營收貢獻來源  
緯穎是緯創 (3231 TT ，NT$188.5，增加持股 )持股 37.68%子公
司，專為超大規模雲端資料中心供應硬體與解決方案，受惠來自
網路內容供應商 (Content Service Provider(CSP)) 客戶之資料中心
擴建需求趨勢，預期隨著人工智慧、自動駕駛及擴增 /虛擬實境對
運算能力要求提高，網路流量將不斷成長，有助於公司來年營收
與獲利持續成長。其 客戶主要為 Meta ( 美)、Microsoft ( 美)與
AWS ( 美)，涉及伺服器、儲存以及 AI 伺服器。

台灣 緯穎

ESG 圖表定義 
項目 定義 說明 
能源使用 
直接與間接能源消耗總量(十億焦耳) 
- 公司運營範圍內消耗之能源總量 
- 能源使用總量 = 直接能源消耗總量 + 間接能源消耗量 
- 總能源使用量包括購買的能源、生產的能源 
- 就公用事業而言，輸電/電網損耗為其業務活動之一部分，被視為總能源消耗，數據不包括
為滿足能源使用而生產的電力（公用事業為出售而生產） 
- 就公用事業而言，用於能源生產的煤、天然氣或核能等原料不屬於「總能源使用」項下

購買之再生能源 
Primary再生能源購買總量(十億焦耳) 
- 公司各種來源之能源消耗量與購買的能源中屬於自然界可再生者（太陽能、風能、水能、
生物質能、地熱能）之量  
- 如無證據顯示再生能源由公司生產，則所報告的能源數據視為購買的再生能源

再生能源使用率 再生能源占總能源消耗量之比例  
CO2 約當排放量 
直接 CO2 與 CO2 約當排放量(公噸) 
- 公司擁有或控制的來源的直接排放量（範圍 1 排放量） 
- 相關氣體：二氧化碳 (CO2)、甲烷 (CH4)、一氧化二氮 (N2O)、氫氟碳化物 (HFCS)、全氟化
合物 (PFCS)、六氟化硫 (SF6)、三氟化氮 (NF3)

CO2 約當排放量營收比 
直接 CO2 與 CO2 約當排放量（公噸）銷售（百萬元）比 
- 公司擁有或控制的來源的直接排放量（範圍 1 排放量） 
- 相關氣體：二氧化碳 (CO2)、甲烷 (CH4)、一氧化二氮 (N2O)、氫氟碳化物 (HFCS)、全氟化
合物 (PFCS)、六氟化硫 (SF6)、三氟化氮 (NF3)

廢棄物總量 
廢棄物總量(公噸) 
- 總廢棄物 = 無害廢棄物 + 有害廢棄物 
- 僅考慮固體廢棄物，但如液體廢棄物以公噸為單位呈報，則會將其加入求得包含液體廢棄
物之總量 
- 對於採礦、石油與天然氣等行業，tailings、廢石、煤、飛灰等廢棄物亦考慮在內

廢棄物回收率 
公司呈報的廢棄物回收率 
- 廢棄物回收率 = 廢棄物回收量/總廢棄物*100 
- 廢棄物轉化為能源或經由廢棄物焚燒產生能源視為廢棄物回收 
- 經由堆肥回收的廢棄物視為回收之廢棄物

總取水量 
總取水量(立方公尺) 
- 由呈報組織直接或經水公司等中介機構從任何水源抽取的總水量 
- 水井、城鎮/公用事業/市政用水、河水、地表水等不同之水源均予以考慮

環保支出 
環保支出總金額 
- 所有用於環境保護，或防止、減少、控制環境因素、影響、危害的投資與支出，亦包括處
置、處理、衛生、清理支出

員工流動率 
員工流動率 
- 包括任何原因（自願或非自願）離開公司的員工，如辭職、退休、自然離職/死亡、醫療失
能、冗員、裁員、重組、解僱、裁減或定期合約到期 
- 員工流動率 =（離開之員工/平均員工人數）*100 
- 平均員工人數 =（本年末員工人數 + 去年末員工人數）/2 
- 本財年末員工數 =上個財年末員工數 + 新員工數 - 離開之員工數

女性經理 
女性經理百分比 
- 女性經理占公司經理的百分比 
- 如有不同階層之百分比，如最高階、高階、中階、初階，則會考慮中階女性經理之百分比 
- 女性經理百分比 = 女性經理人數/經理總數*100

女性員工 
女性員工百分比 
- 女性員工占公司員工總數的百分比 
- 女性員工百分比 = 女性人數/員工總數*100

教育訓練總時數 
所有員工教育訓練總時數 
- 僅考慮員工教育訓練時數 
- 包括一般員工所有類型的教育訓練（如健康與安全、環境、急難事件救援、技能與職業發
展） 
- 如果數據以天為單位，則乘以 8，係假設 1 天 =  8 小時工作

每位員工教育訓練時數 平均每年每位員工總教育訓練時數  
資料來源：Refinitiv；凱基

## Document 581: 凱基投顧_Radar Monthly_消費產業_劉昃恩_20261014

Source PDF: all report pdf/凱基投顧_Radar Monthly_消費產業_劉昃恩_20261014.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-14
Pages: 24
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

十月 14, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R   1

產業報告  台灣

Radar Monthly 
消費 
消費放緩風險揮之不去  
重要訊息  
本期 Radar Monthly －消費產業，我們提供 ：(1) 美國年底購物旺季展望；(2) 
台灣消費產業 9 月營收回顧；與  (3) 3Q26 財報預覽。  
評論及分析  
美國消費指標走低，放大投資者對購物旺季之擔憂。 儘管今年迄今美國零售
銷售展現超乎預期的韌性 ， 全球運動休閒品牌及零售商 2Q26 財報顯示 2H26
庫存去化已成定局，且近期更多指標浮現消費降溫早期跡象。 8 月信用卡及
其他循環信貸餘額減少 48 億美元，創下 2024 年 11 月以來最大單月減幅；
這透露在居高不下的油價與高利率壓力時間拉長下，民眾已從「因漲價預期
而提前囤貨」 轉向 「償還部分卡債」 ， 信用擴張所創造的額外購買力步入收縮。
10 月密大消費者信心指數進一步惡化至 46.3，連續三個月下滑 ，並逼近歷史
低點，反映投資人對即將而來的購物旺季消費力道充滿疑慮。  
9 月營收回顧。 在台灣消費次產業中，貨櫃、散裝及航空業為 9 月營收成長
最快之族群，分別年增 73%、29%、35%，遠優於過去十年同期平均水準。
貨櫃航運主要受美國政府退稅刺激庫存回補帶動，散裝航運得益於美洲穀物
及鐵礦砂旺季出口強勁。A I 相關貨運的高需求挹注航空業成長延續。在外銷
族群中，成衣及製鞋業 9 月營收分別改善至年增 11%、9%，係因低基期與
匯率正面貢獻。受新舊車款過渡期影響，自行車品牌廠 9 月營收年減 4%。 
3Q26 財報預覽 。我們初 步分析 3Q26 財報可望優於預期族群包括貨櫃航運、
航空及隱形眼鏡。貨櫃航商中萬海 (2615 TT，NT$125，增加持股) 表現應最
為突出，主因佔其貨量 比重超過 50%之近洋線運價漲幅超前。航空業將受惠
暑假旅遊旺季及 AI 產品空運出口之雙重加持。隱形眼鏡則因產品組合有利及
產能利用率滿載， 3Q26 利潤率及獲利潛在上檔。整體而言， 3Q26 台幣貶值
對出口導向消費產業將有業外加分。內需方面，台灣 2H26 假期較多將有助
於餐飲業者營收與獲利佳績延伸。  
投資建議  
由於以出口導向為主消費族群可能持續面臨需求放緩及價格壓力，我們建議
關注 3Q26 財報可望優於預期 、 且2027 年獲利成長展望穩健之利基型類股。
凱基偏好個股包括 ： 華航 (2610 TT，NT$20.65 ， 增加持股)、長 榮航 (2618 TT ，
NT$43.4， 增加持股) 、望 隼 (4771 TT，NT$205.5 ， 增加持股)、視 陽 (6782 TT ，
NT$244.5 ，增加持股)、巨大  (9921 TT ，NT$92.1 ，增加持股)、美利達 (9914 
TT，NT$66.7， 增加持股)、 八方雲集 (2753 TT，NT$184， 增加持股)、王 品 (2727 
TT，NT$242，增加持股) 。 
投資風險 
地緣政治衝突擴大；原物料價格驟升 。

焦點內容  
1. 美國近期浮現更多消費降溫之早期
跡象，加深了投資者對購物旺季消費
力道擔憂。 
2. 在台灣消費產業中，貨櫃、散裝及航
空公司 9 月營收成長最快者。 
3. 3Q26 財報可望優於預期之族群包括
貨櫃航運、航空及隱形眼鏡。

凱基投顧

自行車 年增率

營建股 9 月營收大幅成長 
1. 營建股 9 月營收月增 50%、年 增55% ，
成長來自大型建商陸續取照進入交屋認
列期，包括興富發 (2542 TT ，NT$39.7 ，
持有)、遠雄(5 522 TT，NT$61.6 ，未評
等)、愛山林(2542 TT，NT$49.75 ，持有)
等。 
2. 9 月央行理監事會議放寬房市管制 ， 自然
人第二戶房貸成數上限由 60% 放寬至
70%，預期有助房市銷售回溫。

貨櫃航運股 9 月營收續創近兩年新高 
1. 貨櫃航運股 9 月營收月增 4%、年增
73%。 為連續第6 個月月增成長 ， 創2021
年以來最佳紀錄。 
2. 萬海近洋線佔比最高，受惠於 8 月以來
近洋線運價大漲，營收月增幅度達
13.4% ，顯著優於同業。 
3. SCFI 綜合指數 、 歐線 、 美西線、美東線、
近洋線， 7/31 至今累計漲幅分別為
-28%、22%、16%、87%。漲幅集中於
美線及近洋線，為萬海主要航線，預期
獲利上修空間較長榮及陽明更大。  
1.  
台灣自行車廠商 9 月營收持續年減 
1. 台灣自行車廠商合計營收 9 月持續年減
4%， 主因處於新舊年度車款交替的產品轉
換期。 
2. 隨著庫存去化接近尾聲，自行車品牌廠正
準備 4Q26 陸續出貨新車款，我們認為將
有助帶動營收回升。 
3. 巨大（9921 TT，NT$92.1，OP）9 月中國
市場銷售年增 15%，維持雙位數成長；美
利達（9914 TT，NT$66.7，OP）9 月中國
市場出貨年增 6%，主要受惠登山車需求
回溫。

美股焦點

Nike ( 美) 展望低迷  
Nike (美) 公布 1Q FY27 (6 -8 月 ； 財年止於5 月) 營收年減 4%至 112 億美元，
低於市場預估，主因 Nike 運動休閒系列、 Jordan 品牌及大中華區表現疲軟。
考量消費者購買謹慎與客流量不足 ， 管理層預計 FY27 營收將年減高個位數，
隱含 2Q-4Q FY27 營收衰退擴大。因此，公司計畫進一步清理過剩庫存 。繼
Air Jordan 1 (AJ1 ) 及 Dunk 供應減量後，未來 Jordan 特定復古鞋款發售頻率
也將降低，力求恢復供應稀缺模式；這對於豐泰 (9910 TT ，持有，NT$67.5) 
訂單展望將造成負面影響。

 Adidas  
圖 17：摘要   圖 18：Adidas 服飾業務為亮點所在

35%、19%。歐非中東營收年增 7%，反映促銷活動頻繁。 
3. 為在外部環境動盪中確保產品供應，公司提前採購並加快拉貨
速度。2Q26 庫存天數位處高檔水準。

零售

圖 44：零售產業摘要  圖 45：8 月台灣零售銷售成長放緩 
1. 台灣 8 月零售銷售年增 6.5%，維持穩健成長動能，主要受惠中
元普渡及中秋禮盒採買需求、百貨新商場與新品牌加入。其中超
級市場表現最佳，年增 8.7%，百貨公司及便利商店則分別年增 
8.4% 與 2.9%。 
2. 台灣 8 月餐飲業營業年增 1.8%，增幅較上月收斂，主要受農曆
七月影響餐廳婚宴收入，加上降雨天數增加影響來客數，抵銷暑
假假期聚餐需求。 
3. 中國消費動能持續放緩， 8 月餐飲收入年增率由 1.4%降至
1.1%，零售銷售年增率亦由 0.6%收斂至 0.4%。

觀光

圖 52：觀光產業摘要  圖 53：台灣入境旅客增加 
1. 台灣飯店業者合計營收 9 月年增 8%，受惠季節性餐飲需求及休
閒旅遊活動帶動，維持穩健成長。 
2. 台灣 8 月入境旅客人數年減 1.5%。主要客源地中，美洲旅客年
增 11.9%，為各區域中成長最快；歐洲旅客年增 7.7%，亞洲旅
客則年減 3.5%。 
3. 隨觀光需求逐步復甦，台灣飯店 2Q26 平均房價年增 3.8%，延
續成長趨勢，住房率則年增 0.5 個百分點，維持穩定。

成衣 & 製鞋

圖 68：成衣  & 製鞋產業摘要   圖 69：美國 8 月服裝與鞋類零售銷售年增 4% 
1. 美國 8 月服裝與鞋類零售銷售為年增 4%，較 1H26 持續放緩。
考量關稅與中東戰事之不確定性尚存，品牌及零售商維持保守
拉貨，產業存銷比下滑。 
2. 美國 9 月消費者信心指數進一步下滑，主因民眾擔心戰爭驅動
的油價波動及關稅效應將不利於消費前景。 
3. 美國鞋類與服裝進口自中國的比重 1H26 下滑至 19%與 10%，
對比 2019 年分別為 50%、30%。

圖 76 : 營建產業摘要  圖 77：房市成交量低迷

海運

圖 84 : 航運產業摘要    圖 85：亞洲颱風侵襲頻繁，準班率下降  
1. 除陽明、長榮、ZMI(以)尚在規劃中以外，前十大航商均已復航紅海
航道，蘇伊士運河通行量恢復至紅海危機前 50~70%。

價則受到紅海復航影響而走低。

地運力降至 390 萬 TEU，佔全球總運力由 14.6%降至 11.0%。 
4. 散裝運價，北美穀物及亞洲煤炭運輸需求推升 Panamax 及 Handy

航空

圖 92：航空產業摘要    圖 93：航空燃油價格較 6 月低點反彈超過 50% 
1. 客運，9 月暑假結束進入小月，不過長榮航 (2618 TT, NT$43.4, 增
加持股) 及華航 (2610 TT, NT$20.65, 增加持股) 客運收入僅分別月
減 16%、8%，低於過去三年之平均 17%。 
2. 貨運，AI SERVER及半導體出貨需求持續放大，且 9 月起進入傳統
空運旺季，貨運收入顯著成長。 
3. 中東衝突暫無解，航空燃油價格維持於每桶 170~180 美元高檔，
較 6 月低點回升超過 60%，將壓抑航空業 4Q26 獲利爆發性。

圖 100：汽車產業摘要   圖 101：台灣 9 月汽車銷量回升 
1. 台灣 9 月汽車銷量年增 27%至 3.7 萬輛 (優於歷史平均 3.5 萬輛)。
其中，進口車銷量年增 27%，優於國產車的 3%，主要受惠 Tesla 
(美) 大量到港交車，及 Mercedes-Benz (德) 新車款於 9 月集中交
付，帶動兩品牌銷量分別年增 113%及 54%。 
2. Tesla 9 月銷量達 5,298 輛，占整體電動車銷量 75%，帶動整體電
動車銷量達 7,026 輛，電動車滲透率攀升至 19.0%。 
3. 中國 8 月乘用車銷量年減 6.2%，反映內需仍承壓；惟新能源車銷
量年增 17.8%，占新車銷量比重達 60.6%，累計 1-8 月中國自有
品牌市占率提升至 73%。

股價說明 等級 定義 
 增加持股 (OP) 對個股持正面看法，預期個股未來十二個月的表現超越凱基證券集團所追蹤的相關市場的總報酬。 
 持有 (N) 對個股持中性看法，預期個股未來十二個月的表現符合凱基證券集團所追蹤的相關市場的總報酬。 
 降低持股 (U) 對個股持負面看法，預期個股未來十二個月的表現低於凱基證券集團所追蹤的相關市場的總報酬。 
 未評等 (NR) 凱基證券未對該個股加以評等。 
 受法規限制 
未評等 (R) 
受凱基證券集團內部政策和/或相關法令限制使凱基證券集團無法進行某些形式的資訊交流，其中包
括提供評等給投資人參考。 
  *總報酬 = (十二個月目標價-現價)/現價

## Document 642: 宏观点评：三重拐点的信号初现——三季度和9月经济数据点评

Source PDF: all report pdf/宏观点评：三重拐点的信号初现——三季度和9月经济数据点评.pdf
Report source: 东吴证券
Date: 2026-10-20
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

证券研究报告·宏观报告·宏观点评

东吴证券研究所

2026 年 10 月 19 日

证券分析师  芦哲 
执业证书：S0600524110003 
 luzhe@dwzq.com.cn 
证券分析师  占烁 
执业证书：S0600524120005 
 zhansh@dwzq.com.cn

大类资产如何表现？ —— 海外周报
20261019》 
2026-10-19 
《 AI 如何影响我国就业？与美国有
何差异？》 
2026-10-18

[Table_Tag]    [Table_Summary] 
◼ 10 月 19 日，国家统计局发布三季度和 9 月经济数据，内需明显回升，
固投累计同比年内首次止跌。三季度 GDP 实际同比 4.5%，高于 Wind
一致预期 4.4%，较二季度 4.3%回升 0.2 个百分点；前三季度累计同比
增长 4.6%。需求端，9 月出口同比 36.3%（前值 24.9%） ，远高于Wind
一致预期 25.9%；社零同比 1.1%（前值 0.4%） ，高于Wind 一致预期
0.7%； 固定资产投资累计同比-7.2%（前值-7.2%），高于 Wind 一致预期
-7.5%。供给端，9 月工业增加值同比 5.5%（前值 5.2%），持平于 Wind
一致预期 5.5%，服务业生产指数同比 4.8%（前值 4.1%）。 
◼ 核心观点：从三季度经济数据来看， 我们能看到当前经济的三重拐点正
在出现。第一重拐点是旧动能回升的拐点，消费、投资和地产在近期都
有一定改善。消费方面，8-9 月社零同比从 0.4%回升至 1.1%，而服务零
售累计同比也从 4.9%升至 5.0%；房地产方面，一线城市二手房价环比
涨幅从 0.1%回升到 0.2%，70 城新房价格环比从-0.2%升至-0.1%；固投
累计同比今年以来首次终结连续下降趋势，9 月持平于 8 月的-7.2%。 
◼ 第二重拐点是价格周期拐点。持续一年多的 PPI 上行周期可能正在见
顶，9 月 PPI 同比达到 5%，9-10 月大概率将迎来同比顶部。布油现价
在 10 月 8 日一度达到 135.8 美元/桶，10 月 16 日回落至 116.9 美元/桶。
油价已处于历史高点，进一步上行空间有限，PPI 难以继续上攻，近期
将出现顶部。 
◼ 第三重拐点是宏观政策拐点。从 7 月底政治局会议开始，新一轮稳增长
政策逐步落地，8 月京沪两地放松住房限购和公积金贷款，828 房地产
新政推动预售制改革并放松个人住房贷款年限和收入比例，9 月初 8000
亿政策性金融工具发行，928 国常会再部署增量政策，929 中央财政首
次进行房贷贴息，10 月启用 5500 亿地方债结存限额用于补充地方财政
和项目建设。从政策力度来看，可能是 “924”以来边际增量力度最大的
一次稳增长。 
◼ 三重拐点意味着什么？2027 年可以更乐观一些。第一，对消费、投资等
旧动能而言，一些制约因素明显缓解，如房价逐步企稳、“以旧换新”的
基数和透支效应缓解等，这些都有助于内需回升的态势延续到明年。第
二，PPI 见顶后，成本上涨对中下游的挤压也会有所缓解，有助于增强
经济增长的扩散效应。第三，房贷贴息意味着宏观政策开始有新工具，
并且新工具更多面向居民部门，未来仍可扩大范围和力度，政策取向更
加积极。 
◼ 具体来看，三季度和 9 月各项经济指标有以下特点： 
◼ 1、供给端服务业景气度改善幅度大于工业。9 月工业和服务业双双改
善，工业增加值同比从 8 月的 5.2%回升至 5.5%，服务业生产指数从 8
月的 4.1%回升至 4.8%。 
◼ 服务业景气改善主要来自于哪些行业？从统计局发布会来看， 主要包
括：信息传输、 软件和信息技术服务业， 租赁和商务服务业， 交通运输、
仓储和邮政业，住宿和餐饮业生产指数分别增长 9.8%、9.5%、5.4%、
5.3%。 
◼ 工业方面，上游原材料和建材等行业景气度改善。工业分行业来看，采
矿业、化学原料和制品、酒饮茶制造、非金属矿等行业景气改善幅度较
大。这其中，煤炭、电气机械、非金属矿等行业的产能利用率较二季度
明显回升。特别是非金属矿物制品业在 26Q1 达到 56.9%的底部后，近
两个季度连续回升，表明房地产上下游行业出清较为充分，行业供需拐
点将近。 
◼ 2、消费：9 月读数改善，两年平均增速稳定。社零同比从 8 月的 0.4%

### Page 2

2 / 4 
回升到 9 月的 1.1%，其中商品零售从 0.3%回升至 1.0%，餐饮收入从
1.1%回升到 2.1%。 
◼ 近期社零有两个特点：一是两年平均增速非常稳定，自 7 月以来连续三
个月稳定在 2%上下，这表明基数效应是影响近几个月社零同比波动的
主要因素，以此外推 11-12 月社零同比预计能达到 3%左右。 
◼ 二是除汽车外的“以旧换新”商品增速改善。汽车零售同比降幅继续扩
大至 9 月的-21.2%，但除此之外，其他类别零售较低点都已经显著改善。
如家具零售同比从最低今年 4 月的-10.4%回升至 9 月-3%，家电从去年
11 月的-19.4%升至 9 月 1.9%，通讯器材从今年 5 月的 0.7%回升至 9 月
31.1%，建材从去年 11 月的-17%升至 9 月-9.2%。 
◼ 3、投资：今年首次累计同比的“止跌”。9 月固投累计同比持平于 8 月
的-7.2%，我们推算的当月同比在 7 月触及-12.8%的低点后，也已连续
两个月收窄降幅至 9 月的-7.2%。产业投资和基建投资都出现了较大幅
度的改善， 制造业投资 当月 同比 从-6.1%回升至 -0.9%，基建投资 从-
14.7%回升至-10.5%。 
◼ 我们认为，三方面资金力量有助于推动四季度固投进一步止跌回稳。一
是今年前 8 个月特别国债和专项债落地较慢，9 月开始明显加快进度；
二是 9 月初开始落地的 8000 亿政策性金融工具；三是 10 月启用的 5500
亿地方债结存限额，其中 2500 亿用于投资。 
◼ 风险提示：房地产市场后续变化存在不确定性；投资止跌回稳存在反复
风险；地缘政治变化影响物价和外贸。

### Page 4

免责及评级说明部分

东吴证券投资评级标准 
投资评级基于分析师对报告发布日后 6 至 12 个月内行业或公司回报潜力相对基准表现
的预期（A 股市场基准为沪深 300 指数，香港市场基准为恒生指数，美国市场基准为标普 
500 指数， 新三板基准指数为三板成指 （针对协议转让标的） 或三板做市指数 （针对做市转
让标的），北交所基准指数为北证 50 指数），具体如下： 
公司投资评级： 
买入：预期未来 6 个月个股涨跌幅相对基准在 15%以上； 
增持：预期未来 6 个月个股涨跌幅相对基准介于 5%与 15%之间； 
中性：预期未来 6 个月个股涨跌幅相对基准介于-5%与 5%之间； 
减持：预期未来 6 个月个股涨跌幅相对基准介于-15%与-5%之间； 
卖出：预期未来 6 个月个股涨跌幅相对基准在-15%以下。 
行业投资评级： 
增持： 预期未来 6 个月内，行业指数相对强于基准 5%以上； 
中性： 预期未来 6 个月内，行业指数相对基准-5%与 5%； 
减持： 预期未来 6 个月内，行业指数相对弱于基准 5%以上。 
我们在此提醒您， 不同证券研究机构采用不同的评级术语及评级标准。 我们采用的是相
对评级体系， 表示投资的相对比重建议。 投资者买入或者卖出证券的决定应当充分考虑自身
特定状况， 如具体投资目的、 财务状况以及特定需求等， 并完整理解和使用本报告内容， 不
应视本报告为做出投资决策的唯一因素。

2026年４～６月期の実質ＧＤＰ成長率◆設備投資とその他内需が上方改定
４～６月期の２次ＱＥでは 、実質ＧＤＰ成長
率は前期比年率＋ 1.4％と、１次ＱＥの同＋
1.1％から上方改定。設備投資とその他 内需の上
方改定が全体を押し上げ。
◆製造業の生産活動は回復基調
８月の鉱工業生産は 、前月比＋0.1％と小幅に
増加。輸送用機械工業が減少したものの 、堅調な
ＡＩ関連需要に伴い 、半導体製造装置などの生
産用機械工業を中心に増加。
先行きの生産計画によると 、９月の製造工業生
産予測指数（過去の予測誤差を基に算出した補
正値）は同＋3.1％と上昇する見込み。ＡＩ関連
などの半導体需要を背景に、機械工業が大幅に増
産する見通し。
◆輸出は持ち直し
輸出は回復基調。米国、アジアなどの輸出が堅調
に推移していることに加え、中東危機により落ち込ん
でいた中東向けの輸出が足元で持ち直し。
◆消費者マインドは改善
８月の消費者態度指数は４ヵ月連続の上昇。食
料などを中心とした物価の落ち着きを受け 、購買力

が改善しており、暮らし向きや耐久消費財の買い時
判断に対する意識が改善。

### Page 5

日本
(株)日本総合研究所 日本経済展望 2026年9月4
企業収益は堅調、設備投資は持ち直し
◆企業収益は堅調
法人企業統計によると 、2026年４～６月期の
経常利益（全産業）は前年比＋24.6%と、７四
半期連続の増益。価格転嫁の進展などにより売上
高の大幅増が経常利益を押し上げたほか 、円安基
調が続くなか 、製造業では海外子会社からの配当
など（営業外損益に計上 ）が増加したことも増益
要因に。中東情勢の混乱によって 、中小企業で原
材料価格などの変動費増加が収益の下押し要因と
なったものの、企業収益全体への影響は限定的。
先行きを展望すると、個人消費の底堅さや価格転
嫁の進展などを背景に 、経常利益は増勢を維持す
る見通し。
◆金利上昇が設備投資の重石となるリスク
法人企業統計によると、設備投資（季調値、ソフ
トウェア投資を含む）は前期比＋1.5％と、２四半
期ぶりの増加。ソフトウェア投資が前期から減少した
ものの、既存設備の維持・更新需要などを背景とし
たその他の投資の増加が全体を押し上げ 。人手不

足を背景とした省力化やＤＸ化への投資需要は根
強く、先行きのソフトウェア投資は堅調に推移する見
通し。
ただし、金利上昇に伴う企業の借入負担の増加
が懸念材料。宿泊・飲食や不動産、電気・ガスなど
の産業では、総資産の約50%を有利子負債に依
存。これらの産業では 、返済負担の高まりが新たな
設備投資の重石となる可能性。

### Page 6

日本
(株)日本総合研究所 日本経済展望 2026年9月5
堅調な所得環境が個人消費を下支え
◆実質賃金は堅調に増加
７月の一般労働者の所定内給与は前年比＋
4.0％と、前月から伸びを拡大。パートタイム労働者
（時給ベース）は同＋5.9％と、高めの伸びに。
夏季賞与をみても前年比ベースで堅調に増加し、
大企業の総平均支給額は 100万円を突破。非製
造業は90万円台と、前年比＋９％近くの高い伸び
に。給与と賞与の増加を受け 、雇用者全体の実質
賃金も前年比＋1.9%と上昇。
先行き、実質賃金はプラス圏で推移する見通し 。
人手不足感の高まりや物価の高騰などにより企業に
対する賃上げ圧力は強く 、名目賃金は高めの伸び
を維持すると予想 。また、政府のエネルギー補助金
などにより物価上昇圧力が一定程度緩和することが
実質賃金の改善に寄与する見込み。
◆実質賃金の増加で消費も伸長
消費活動指数を形態別にみると 、エアコンの駆け
込み需要や自動車の減税などが影響し 、耐久財を
中心に足元では財消費が持ち直し 。加えて、娯楽

関連を中心にサービス消費も堅調に推移。
先行き、実質賃金の増加を背景に 、個人消費は
緩やかに増加する見込み。ただし、資源価格の高騰
により物価上昇圧力が強まった場合 、実質賃金の
下振れを通じて消費が腰折れする可能性に注意。
